    name: str

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]: ...


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/beaconing.py
# ==========================================
"""
BeaconingDetector — периодические обращения к C2 (heartbeat).

Признак: множество TCP-соединений к одной паре (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port)
с регулярным интервалом. C2-heartbeat — запрограммированный таймер
с малым jitter.

Пример: заражённый хост каждые 60 секунд (±2) стучится на C2.
Соединения короткие (heartbeat), но регулярные.

Логика (0.7.0):
- Группировка FlowKey по (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port).
- N >= beaconing_min_connections (20).
- Длительность >= beaconing_min_duration_sec (600).
- jitter = stddev(intervals) / mean(intervals) < 0.1.
- Средний размер flow < beaconing_max_avg_bytes (5000).
- Rate = count / duration_min <= beaconing_max_flows_per_minute (5).
- stddev(payload)/mean(payload) <= beaconing_payload_stddev_ratio_max (0.5).
- dst_ip не internal (по флагу beaconing_skip_internal_dst).
- dst_port не в mail-портах (POP3/IMAP).
- Группа не multi-tenant: <= 1 уникального SNI/Host.
- Единственный SNI/Host не в allowlist.

Что отсеивается:
- Mail polling (POP3/IMAP) — порт-фильтр.
- Chrome keepalive к CDN/analytics — multi-tenant filter +
  SNI/Host allowlist.
- Windows Update, Apple CDN — SNI/Host allowlist.
- Burst-соединения (Chrome parallel fetch) — rate check.
- Browser keepalive с разным payload — payload variance.

Что остаётся:
- C2 на одном домене/IP: один SNI (или нет SNI), const payload,
  низкий jitter, долгий горизонт.

Confidence: 0.85 при jitter → 0, 0.6 при jitter → max_jitter_ratio.

Отличие от brute_force:
  - brute_force — много flows к одной цели ЗА КОРОТКОЕ ВРЕМЯ.
  - beaconing   — много flows к одной цели с РАВНЫМИ ИНТЕРВАЛАМИ
                  на длинном горизонте.

Отличие от port_scan:
  - port_scan — много разных dst на один порт.
  - beaconing — много flows к ОДНОЙ паре (dst_ip, dst_port).
"""
import statistics
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding
from paradox.net_utils import is_internal_ip


@dataclass
class _BeaconGroup:
    first_timestamps: list[float] = field(default_factory=list)
    per_flow_bytes: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    sni_set: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    host_set: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    total_bytes: int = 0
    total_packets: int = 0
    count: int = 0

    @property
    def duration(self) -> float:
        if len(self.first_timestamps) < 2:
            return 0.0
        return max(self.first_timestamps) - min(self.first_timestamps)

    def avg_bytes(self) -> float:
        if self.count == 0:
            return 0.0
        return self.total_bytes / self.count

    def payload_stddev_ratio(self) -> float | None:
        """
        stddev/mean по размеру payload.

        C2: const payload → 0.0.
        Browser: разные запросы → > 0.5.

        Возвращает None, если данных < 5 flow.
        """
        if len(self.per_flow_bytes) < 5:
            return None
        mean = statistics.mean(self.per_flow_bytes)
        if mean <= 0:
            return None
        return statistics.pstdev(self.per_flow_bytes) / mean

    def unique_destinations(self) -> set[str]:
        """
        Уникальные SNI + HTTP Host в группе.

        0 — нет ни SNI, ни Host (raw IP, возможно C2).
        1 — один домен (C2 ИЛИ однодоменный сервис).
        >1 — shared CDN/edge (Chrome к Akamai, multi-tenant edge).
        """
        return self.sni_set | self.host_set


def _suffix_match(value: str, suffixes: frozenset[str]) -> bool:
    """
    endswith-проверка по доменному суффиксу.

    'www.google-analytics.com' matches 'google-analytics.com'.
    'notgoogle-analytics.com' does NOT match (нужна точка перед).
    """
    if not value:
        return False
    v = value.lower().rstrip(".")
    return any(v == s or v.endswith("." + s) for s in suffixes)


class BeaconingDetector:
    name = "beaconing"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        min_conn = config.beaconing_min_connections
        min_duration = config.beaconing_min_duration_sec
        max_jitter = config.beaconing_max_jitter_ratio
        max_avg_bytes = config.beaconing_max_avg_bytes
        max_rate = config.beaconing_max_flows_per_minute
        max_payload_var = config.beaconing_payload_stddev_ratio_max
        skip_internal = config.beaconing_skip_internal_dst
        mail_ports = config.beaconing_mail_polling_ports
        allow_suffixes = config.beaconing_allowlist_suffixes
        allow_ips = config.beaconing_allowlist_ips

        groups: dict[tuple[str, str, int], _BeaconGroup] = defaultdict(
            _BeaconGroup
        )

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.proto != "tcp":
                continue
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            if flow.first_ts is None:
                continue

            # Mail-порты: POP3/IMAP поллинг = design, не C2.
            # SMTP (25/465/587) НЕ здесь — бывает C2-канал.
            if key.dst_port in mail_ports:
                continue

            # Точечный IP allowlist (analytics без SNI).
            if key.dst_ip in allow_ips:
                continue

            # internal → internal — отдельная история.
            if skip_internal and is_internal_ip(key.dst_ip, config):
                continue

            group = groups[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, key.dst_port)]
            group.first_timestamps.append(flow.first_ts)
            group.per_flow_bytes.append(flow.bytes_sent)
            group.total_bytes += flow.bytes_sent
            group.total_packets += flow.packets
            group.count += 1
            if flow.sni:
                group.sni_set.add(flow.sni.lower())
            if flow.http_host:
                group.host_set.add(flow.http_host.lower())

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port), g in groups.items():
            if g.count < min_conn:
                continue
            if g.duration < min_duration:
                continue

            # Multi-tenant filter: > 1 уникального SNI/Host = shared edge.
            # Chrome к Akamai открывает десятки соединений к одному IP,
            # но каждый — с разным SNI. C2 использует ОДИН домен.
            unique_dests = g.unique_destinations()
            if len(unique_dests) > 1:
                continue

            # SNI/Host allowlist: один dest, но известный CDN/analytics.
            if len(unique_dests) == 1 and allow_suffixes:
                (single_dest,) = unique_dests
                if _suffix_match(single_dest, allow_suffixes):
                    continue

            # Rate check: Chrome параллельно открывает пачку.
            duration_min = g.duration / 60.0
            if duration_min <= 0:
                continue
            flow_rate = g.count / duration_min
            if flow_rate > max_rate:
                continue

            ts_sorted = sorted(g.first_timestamps)
            intervals = [
                ts_sorted[i + 1] - ts_sorted[i]
                for i in range(len(ts_sorted) - 1)
            ]
            intervals = [iv for iv in intervals if iv > 0]
            if len(intervals) < min_conn - 1:
                continue

            mean_iv = statistics.mean(intervals)
            if mean_iv <= 0:
                continue
            stdev_iv = statistics.pstdev(intervals)
            jitter = stdev_iv / mean_iv

            if jitter >= max_jitter:
                continue

            avg_bytes = g.avg_bytes()
            if avg_bytes > max_avg_bytes:
                continue

            payload_var = g.payload_stddev_ratio()
            if payload_var is not None and payload_var > max_payload_var:
                continue

            confidence = 0.6 + 0.25 * (1 - jitter / max_jitter)
            confidence = max(0.6, min(0.85, confidence))

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="beaconing",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:{dst_port}",
                confidence=round(confidence, 3),
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "count": g.count,
                    "duration_sec": round(g.duration, 1),
                    "flow_rate_per_min": round(flow_rate, 2),
                    "mean_interval_sec": round(mean_iv, 2),
                    "stdev_interval_sec": round(stdev_iv, 3),
                    "jitter_ratio": round(jitter, 3),
                    "avg_bytes_per_conn": round(avg_bytes, 1),
                    "payload_stddev_ratio": (
                        round(payload_var, 3)
                        if payload_var is not None
                        else None
                    ),
                    "unique_destinations": sorted(unique_dests),
                    "total_bytes": g.total_bytes,
                    "total_packets": g.total_packets,
                },
                first_seen=ts_sorted[0],
                last_seen=ts_sorted[-1],
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/brute_force.py
# ==========================================
"""
BruteForceDetector — поиск перебора к одной паре dst_ip:dst_port.

Сигнал: много коротких flows с одного src_ip к одной цели.

Не путать с:
  - port scan       → много dst_port, по 1-3 пакета
  - длинной сессией → 1 flow, много пакетов
  - обычным HTTP    → flows распределены по времени, разных dst
  - трафиком к DC   → LDAP/Kerberos/SMB — это норма
  - Google Safe Browsing → много flows с разными URI
  - браузером к CDN → массовые TCP-соединения на 443

Логика:
  1. Группируем flows по (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port).
  2. Отсеиваем DC-порты (LDAP/Kerberos/SMB).
  3. Отсеиваем TLS-порты (443/8443) — браузер к CDN, не подбор.
  4. Считаем: flow_count, unique_src_ports, avg_packets,
     уникальные URI после нормализации (strip query string).
  5. Отсеиваем по ratio unique_uris / flow_count (много разных
     URI = нормальная работа, не подбор).
  6. Отсеиваем по rate: слишком редко → поллинг.
  7. Отсеиваем по login-hint: HTTP-трафик без login endpoint
     в URI — браузер качает картинки, не подбор.
  8. Пороги: count >= min_flows, avg_packets <= max_avg,
     unique_src_ports >= min_unique_src_ports.

Каждая попытка аутентификации = новый src_port.
Поэтому unique_src_ports ≈ count flows — второй независимый
признак перебора, а не длинной сессии.

Известное ограничение: SYN-скан с одного src на один dst:port
тоже похож на этот паттерн. Отличить можно только по наличию
SYN+ACK — этого сигнала в FlowStats нет. См. TODO.
"""
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    """Агрегат flows для пары (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port)."""
    flow_count: int = 0
    packets_total: int = 0
    bytes_sent_total: int = 0
    src_ports: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)
    uris: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None

    def touch(self, ts: float | None) -> None:
        if ts is None:
            return
        if self.first_seen is None or ts < self.first_seen:
            self.first_seen = ts
        if self.last_seen is None or ts > self.last_seen:
            self.last_seen = ts


def _normalize_uri(uri: str | None) -> str | None:
    """Срезать query string для сравнения endpoint'ов."""
    if not uri:
        return None
    if "?" in uri:
        uri = uri.split("?", 1)[0]
    return uri or None


class BruteForceDetector:
    name = "brute_force"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        excluded_ports = config.brute_force_excluded_ports
        skip_tls_ports = config.brute_force_skip_tls_ports
        login_hints = config.login_endpoint_hints

        agg: dict[tuple[str, str, int], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            if key.proto != "tcp":
                continue
            # DC-порты: много коротких запросов к DC — норма.
            if key.dst_port in excluded_ports:
                continue

            bucket = agg[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, key.dst_port)]
            bucket.flow_count += 1
            bucket.packets_total += flow.packets
            bucket.bytes_sent_total += flow.bytes_sent
            if key.src_port is not None:
                bucket.src_ports.add(key.src_port)

            uri = _normalize_uri(flow.http_uri)
            if uri:
                bucket.uris.add(uri)

            bucket.touch(flow.first_ts)
            bucket.touch(flow.last_ts)

        findings: list[Finding] = []

        for (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port), bucket in agg.items():
            flow_count = bucket.flow_count
            if flow_count < config.brute_force_min_flows:
                continue

            avg_packets = bucket.packets_total / flow_count
            if avg_packets > config.brute_force_max_avg_packets:
                continue

            unique_src_ports = len(bucket.src_ports)
            if unique_src_ports < config.brute_force_min_unique_src_ports:
                continue

            # --- Фильтр 1: ratio уникальных URI ---
            # Много разных URI = нормальная работа (браузер).
            unique_uris = bucket.uris
            if (unique_uris
                    and len(unique_uris) / flow_count
                    > config.brute_force_uri_ratio_threshold):
                continue

            duration = 0.0
            if bucket.first_seen is not None and bucket.last_seen is not None:
                duration = bucket.last_seen - bucket.first_seen

            # --- Фильтр 2: rate ---
            # Слишком редко = поллинг, не подбор.
            if duration > 0:
                rate = flow_count / duration
                if rate < config.brute_force_min_rate_flows_per_sec:
                    continue

            # --- Фильтр 3: TLS-порты ---
            # 443/8443 — массовые TCP-соединения = браузер к CDN.
            if dst_port in skip_tls_ports:
                continue

            # --- Фильтр 4: login endpoint в URI ---
            # Если HTTP-трафик и ни один URI не похож на login
            # endpoint — это не подбор пароля. Если URI нет совсем
            # (SSH/FTP/RDP) — фильтр не применяется.
            if unique_uris:
                has_login = any(
                    hint in uri.lower()
                    for uri in unique_uris
                    for hint in login_hints
                )
                if not has_login:
                    continue

            # --- Confidence ---
            flow_score = min(1.0, flow_count / 100.0)
            port_score = min(1.0, unique_src_ports / 50.0)
            confidence = round(0.5 + 0.3 * flow_score + 0.15 * port_score, 3)
            confidence = min(confidence, 0.95)

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="brute_force",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:{dst_port}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "flow_count": flow_count,
                    "unique_src_ports": unique_src_ports,
                    "unique_uris": len(unique_uris),
                    "packets_total": bucket.packets_total,
                    "bytes_sent_total": bucket.bytes_sent_total,
                    "avg_packets_per_flow": round(avg_packets, 2),
                    "duration_sec": round(duration, 2),
                    "rate_flows_per_sec": (
                        round(flow_count / duration, 6)
                        if duration > 0 else None
                    ),
                },
                first_seen=bucket.first_seen,
                last_seen=bucket.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/cloud_tunnel.py
# ==========================================
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class CloudTunnelDetector:
    """
    Домены на сервисах, которые часто используются для скрытия C2:
    trycloudflare, ngrok, serveo, localtunnel и т.п.
    """
    name = "cloud_tunnel"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        findings: list[Finding] = []

        for name, count in state.dns_queries.items():
            match = self._match_tunnel(name, config)
            if match is None:
                continue

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="cloud_tunnel_domain",
                value=name,
                confidence=0.7,
                evidence={
                    "matched_suffix": match,
                    "queries": count,
                },
            ))

        return findings

    def _match_tunnel(self, name: str, config: Config) -> str | None:
        for suffix in config.cloud_tunnel_domains:
            if name == suffix or name.endswith("." + suffix):
                return suffix
        return None


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/command_injection.py
# ==========================================
"""
CommandInjectionDetector — RCE через HTTP URI или POST body.

Работает поверх state.http_objects (заполняется HTTPObjectExtractor
из склеенных TCP-потоков). Ищет паттерны команд и языковые
конструкции в:
  - URI запроса (query string, path)
  - body запроса (form-urlencoded декодируется)

Признаки RCE:
  - io.popen / os.execute (Lua)
  - system( / exec( / shell_exec( (PHP)
  - os.system / subprocess (Python)
  - $(...) / backticks (shell)
  - cmd.exe / /bin/sh + wget/curl (классика)

Агрегация по (src_ip, uri). Один запрос с RCE = один finding.
Если паттерн найден в N телах — count = N, но finding один.

Ложные срабатывания:
  - HTML/JS в body содержит eval() — фильтруем по kind="request"
  - JSON с "system" в значении — может пройти, если нет "(".
    Поэтому "system(" (со скобкой) — high confidence.

Confidence:
  - high-паттерн → 0.9
  - только medium-паттерны (≥2) → 0.75
  - только один medium → 0.6
"""
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from urllib.parse import unquote


from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    count: int = 0
    matched_high: Counter = field(default_factory=Counter)
    matched_medium: Counter = field(default_factory=Counter)
    body_samples: list[bytes] = field(default_factory=list)
    methods: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    hosts: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    dst_ips: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    dst_ports: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None


class CommandInjectionDetector:
    name = "command_injection"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        high = config.command_injection_patterns_high
        medium = config.command_injection_patterns_medium
        min_patterns = config.command_injection_min_patterns

        buckets: dict[tuple[str, str], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, obj in (state.http_objects or []):
            if obj.kind != "request":
                continue

            # Текст для поиска: URI + body (декодированный)
            haystack_parts: list[str] = []
            if obj.uri:
                haystack_parts.append(obj.uri)
                # декодированный вариант — %3d → "=", %2f → "/"
                try:
                    haystack_parts.append(unquote(obj.uri))
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if obj.body:
                # iso-8859-1 — все 256 байт → символы, безопасно для поиска
                raw_text = obj.body.decode("iso-8859-1", errors="ignore")
                haystack_parts.append(raw_text)
                # url-decode (form-urlencoded)
                try:
                    haystack_parts.append(unquote(raw_text))
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if not haystack_parts:
                continue

            haystack = "\n".join(haystack_parts)
            haystack_lc = haystack.lower()

            found_high: list[str] = [
                p for p in high if p.lower() in haystack_lc
            ]
            found_medium: list[str] = [
                p for p in medium if p.lower() in haystack_lc
            ]

            total = len(found_high) + len(found_medium)
            if total < min_patterns:
                continue
            # Если ни одного high, требуем хотя бы 2 medium —
            # одна подозрительная строка слишком шумно.
            if not found_high and len(found_medium) < 2:
                continue

            # Ключ агрегации: (src_ip, uri)
            agg_key = (key.src_ip, obj.uri or "")
            b = buckets[agg_key]
            b.count += 1
            for p in found_high:
                b.matched_high[p] += 1
            for p in found_medium:
                b.matched_medium[p] += 1
            if len(b.body_samples) < 3 and obj.body:
                b.body_samples.append(obj.body[:256])
            if obj.method:
                b.methods.add(obj.method)
            if obj.headers and obj.headers.get("host"):
                b.hosts.add(obj.headers["host"])
            if key.dst_ip:
                b.dst_ips.add(key.dst_ip)
            if key.dst_port is not None:
                b.dst_ports.add(key.dst_port)

            # Заполняем timestamps из flow.
            # Один URI может прийти через несколько flows —
            # берём min(first_ts) и max(last_ts).
            flow = state.flows.get(key)
            if flow is not None:
                if flow.first_ts is not None:
                    if b.first_seen is None or flow.first_ts < b.first_seen:
                        b.first_seen = flow.first_ts
                if flow.last_ts is not None:
                    if b.last_seen is None or flow.last_ts > b.last_seen:
                        b.last_seen = flow.last_ts

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, uri), b in buckets.items():
            if b.matched_high:
                confidence = 0.9
            elif sum(b.matched_medium.values()) >= 2:
                confidence = 0.75
            else:
                confidence = 0.6

            all_patterns: list[str] = sorted(
                set(b.matched_high) | set(b.matched_medium)
            )
            dst_ips_sorted = sorted(b.dst_ips)
            evidence = {
                "src_ip": src_ip,
                "uri": uri,
                "count": b.count,
                "matched_high": sorted(b.matched_high.keys()),
                "matched_medium": sorted(b.matched_medium.keys()),
                "matched_patterns": all_patterns,
                "methods": sorted(b.methods),
                "hosts": sorted(b.hosts)[:5],
                "dst_ip": dst_ips_sorted[0] if dst_ips_sorted else None,
                "dst_ips": dst_ips_sorted,
                "dst_ports": sorted(b.dst_ports),
            }
            if b.body_samples:
                evidence["body_samples"] = [
                    s.decode("iso-8859-1", errors="replace")[:200]
                    for s in b.body_samples
                ]

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="command_injection",
                value=f"{src_ip} → {uri}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence=evidence,
                first_seen=b.first_seen,
                last_seen=b.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/dga.py
# ==========================================
import math
from collections import Counter

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


def shannon_entropy(s: str) -> float:
    if not s:
        return 0.0
    counts = Counter(s)
    n = len(s)
    return -sum((c / n) * math.log2(c / n) for c in counts.values())


class DGADetector:
    name = "dga"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        findings: list[Finding] = []

        for name, count in state.dns_queries.items():
            if self._is_allowlisted(name, config):
                continue

            label = name.split(".")[0]

            if len(label) < config.dga_min_label_length:
                continue

            # DGA-домены почти никогда не содержат дефисов.
            # Дефис — признак человекочитаемого домена
            # (safebrowsing-cache.google.com и т.п.).
            if "-" in label:
                continue

            entropy = shannon_entropy(label)
            if entropy < config.dga_entropy_threshold:
                continue

            confidence = min(
                0.95,
                0.5 + (entropy - config.dga_entropy_threshold) / 2.0,
            )

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="dga_domain",
                value=name,
                confidence=round(confidence, 3),
                evidence={
                    "label": label,
                    "entropy": round(entropy, 3),
                    "queries": count,
                },
            ))

        return findings

    def _is_allowlisted(self, name: str, config: Config) -> bool:
        from paradox.domain_utils import matches_suffix
        return matches_suffix(name, config.dga_allowlist)


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/dns_tunneling.py
# ==========================================
"""
DNSTunnelingDetector — DNS-туннелирование (exfiltration через DNS).

Признак: длинные уникальные поддомены под одним базовым доменом
от одного клиента. Данные кодируются в qname (base64, hex, custom).

Пример:
  aGVsbG8gd29ybGQ.dns.attacker.com
  d29ybGQgaGVsbG8.dns.attacker.com
  ...

Отличие от DGA:
  - DGA — короткие поддомены (10-15 символов), много разных
  - Tunneling — длинные поддомены (>50), тоже много
Требуем min_qname_length, чтобы DGA не сработал как tunneling.

Отличие от cloud_tunnel_domain:
  - cloud_tunnel_domain — известные сервисы (trycloudflare, ngrok)
  - DNS tunneling — самодельный протокол через DNS

Confidence: 0.9 при длинных qname + много запросов, 0.7 иначе.
"""
from collections import defaultdict

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


def _extract_base_domain(qname: str) -> str:
    """v0.9.0: учитывает двухуровневые TLD (co.uk, com.au)."""
    from paradox.domain_utils import base_domain
    return base_domain(qname)


class DNSTunnelingDetector:
    name = "dns_tunneling"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        min_len = config.dns_tunneling_min_qname_length
        min_long = config.dns_tunneling_min_long_qnames
        min_queries = config.dns_tunneling_min_queries
        allowlist = config.dns_tunneling_allowlist

        findings: list[Finding] = []

        # Группируем по (src_ip, base_domain)
        # value: set[qname] и total_queries
        groups: dict[tuple[str, str], dict] = defaultdict(
            lambda: {"qnames": set(), "total_queries": 0, "long_qnames": set()}
        )

        for src_ip, queries in state.dns_queries_by_src.items():
            for qname, count in queries.items():
                qname_lc = qname.lower().strip(".")
                if not qname_lc:
                    continue
                base = _extract_base_domain(qname_lc)

                # Allowlist (DoH и пр.) — пропускаем
                if any(
                    qname_lc == a or qname_lc.endswith("." + a)
                    for a in allowlist
                ):
                    continue

                key = (src_ip, base)
                groups[key]["qnames"].add(qname_lc)
                groups[key]["total_queries"] += count
                if len(qname_lc) >= min_len:
                    groups[key]["long_qnames"].add(qname_lc)

        for (src_ip, base), data in groups.items():
            if data["total_queries"] < min_queries:
                continue
            if len(data["long_qnames"]) < min_long:
                continue

            long_count = len(data["long_qnames"])
            total_unique = len(data["qnames"])

            confidence = 0.9 if long_count >= min_long * 2 else 0.7

            # Сэмпл длинных qname (первые 5 по алфавиту)
            samples = sorted(data["long_qnames"])[:5]

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="dns_tunneling",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {base}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "base_domain": base,
                    "total_queries": data["total_queries"],
                    "unique_qnames": total_unique,
                    "long_qnames_count": long_count,
                    "min_qname_length": min_len,
                    "samples": samples,
                },
                first_seen=None,
                last_seen=None,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/explainer.py
# ==========================================
"""Автоматическое объяснение уверенности (confidence) детекторов.

Функция build_reasons принимает evidence и возвращает список строк,
описывающих, на основании чего детектор сделал вывод.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

# Словарь: ключ в evidence -> человекочитаемое название
LABELS = {
    "packets_total": "Всего пакетов",
    "unique_src_ports": "Уникальных src-портов",
    "unique_dst_ports": "Уникальных dst-портов",
    "flow_count": "Количество потоков",
    "bytes_total": "Всего байт",
    "attempts": "Попыток",
    "queries": "Запросов",
    "nxdomain_count": "NXDOMAIN-ответов",
    "duration": "Длительность (сек)",
    "packets": "Пакетов",
    "bytes_sent": "Байт отправлено",
    "bytes_recv": "Байт получено",
    "dst_ports": "Целевых портов",
    "src_ips": "Уникальных источников",
    "dst_ips": "Уникальных назначений",
    "domains": "Доменов",
    "entropy": "Энтропия",
    "payload_size": "Размер payload",
    "sample": "Пример",
    "rule": "YARA-правило",
    "tags": "Теги",
    "filename": "Имя файла",
    "ja3": "JA3-отпечаток",
    "ja3s": "JA3S-отпечаток",
    "sni": "SNI",
    "user_agent": "User-Agent",
    "host": "Хост",
    "uri": "URI",
    "method": "HTTP-метод",
}

def build_reasons(evidence: dict) -> list[str]:
    if not evidence:
        return []

    reasons = []
    # 1. Известные ключи
    for k, v in evidence.items():
        if k in LABELS:
            reasons.append(f"{LABELS[k]}: {v}")

    # 2. Если ничего не нашли (неизвестные ключи) — выводим первые 3 пары
    if not reasons:
        for k, v in list(evidence.items())[:3]:
            reasons.append(f"{k}: {v}")

    return reasons


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/ftp_brute_force.py
# ==========================================
"""
FTPBruteForceDetector — перебор паролей к FTP-серверу.

Сигнал: много flows к одной паре (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port=21) с
уникальными src_ports. Каждая попытка логина = новое TCP-соединение
(новый src_port), потом закрытие после отказа. 100+ попыток подряд —
однозначный словарный перебор.

Отличие от HTTP-brute_force:
  - HTTP-детектор рубит avg_packets > 20 (FTP-сессия = 1185 пакетов)
  - HTTP-детектор требует login-hint в URI, у FTP URI нет

Confidence:
  - 0.95 при flow_count >= 100
  - 0.85 при flow_count >= 50
  - 0.75 иначе (но >= ftp_brute_force_min_flows)
"""
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    flow_count: int = 0
    src_ports: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)
    packets_total: int = 0
    bytes_total: int = 0
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None

    def touch(self, ts: float | None) -> None:
        if ts is None:
            return
        if self.first_seen is None or ts < self.first_seen:
            self.first_seen = ts
        if self.last_seen is None or ts > self.last_seen:
            self.last_seen = ts


class FTPBruteForceDetector:
    name = "ftp_brute_force"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        min_flows = config.ftp_brute_force_min_flows
        min_ports = config.ftp_brute_force_min_unique_src_ports

        agg: dict[tuple[str, str], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.proto != "tcp":
                continue
            if key.dst_port != 21:
                continue

            bucket = agg[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip)]
            bucket.flow_count += 1
            if key.src_port is not None:
                bucket.src_ports.add(key.src_port)
            bucket.packets_total += flow.packets
            bucket.bytes_total += flow.bytes_sent
            bucket.touch(flow.first_ts)
            bucket.touch(flow.last_ts)

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, dst_ip), bucket in agg.items():
            if bucket.flow_count < min_flows:
                continue
            if len(bucket.src_ports) < min_ports:
                continue

            if bucket.flow_count >= 100:
                confidence = 0.95
            elif bucket.flow_count >= 50:
                confidence = 0.85
            else:
                confidence = 0.75

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="ftp_brute_force",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:21",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": 21,
                    "flow_count": bucket.flow_count,
                    "unique_src_ports": len(bucket.src_ports),
                    "packets_total": bucket.packets_total,
                    "bytes_total": bucket.bytes_total,
                },
                first_seen=bucket.first_seen,
                last_seen=bucket.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/ip_check_service.py
# ==========================================
"""
IPCheckServiceDetector — обращения к сервисам проверки внешнего IP
(ip-api.com, ifconfig.me, ipinfo.io). Типичный шаг malware и скриптов
для подтверждения интернет-доступа перед C2.

Матчинг по четырём источникам:
  - DNS-запрос к домену сервиса (state.dns_queries)
  - SNI в TLS ClientHello (flow.sni)
  - HTTP Host в запросе (flow.http_host)
  - Прямой IP-запрос (flow.dst_ip в config.ip_check_service_ips)

Все источники дают одно и то же value — каноническое имя сервиса
(например, "ip-api.com"). Если клиент резолвит домен через DNS и потом
стучится на известный IP — все три источника сольются в один finding
с sources={dns, sni, direct_ip}. Это делает результат стабильным.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class IPCheckServiceDetector:
    name = "ip_check_service"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        services = config.ip_check_services
        service_ips = config.ip_check_service_ips

        # value (имя сервиса) -> {sources: set, extra: dict}
        hits: dict[str, dict] = {}

        def _touch(value: str, source: str, extra: dict | None = None) -> None:
            h = hits.setdefault(value, {"sources": set(), "extra": {}})
            h["sources"].add(source)
            if extra:
                h["extra"].update(extra)

        # 1. DNS
        for qname, count in state.dns_queries.items():
            domain = qname.lower().strip(".")
            if domain in services:
                _touch(domain, "dns", {"queries": count})

        # 2. SNI / 3. HTTP Host / 4. direct IP
        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if flow.sni:
                sni = flow.sni.lower().strip(".")
                if sni in services:
                    _touch(sni, "sni",
                           {"flow": f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}"})

            if flow.http_host:
                host = flow.http_host.split(":", 1)[0].lower().strip(".")
                if host in services:
                    _touch(host, "http_host",
                           {"flow": f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}"})

            # direct IP — используем каноническое имя сервиса как value,
            # чтобы finding слился с DNS/SNI-находками
            service_name = service_ips.get(key.dst_ip)
            if service_name:
                _touch(service_name, "direct_ip",
                       {"flow": f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}"})

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for value, h in hits.items():
            findings.append(Finding(
                type="ip_check_service",
                value=value,
                confidence=0.7,
                evidence={
                    "sources": sorted(h["sources"]),
                    **h["extra"],
                },
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: f.value)
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/ja3_malware.py
# ==========================================
"""
JA3MalwareDetector — известные JA3-отпечатки malware/pentest.

Проверяет flow.ja3 (ClientHello fingerprint) против словаря
известных хешей в config.ja3_known_malware. Если совпал — finding
ja3_malware с именем инструмента.

Агрегация по JA3: несколько flow'ов с одним отпечатком — один finding
с перечнем уникальных src/dst.

Confidence 0.85 — известный отпечаток malware это сильный сигнал,
но возможны совпадения с легитимными приложениями, использующими
те же библиотеки (python-requests, go-http-client).
"""
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class JA3MalwareDetector:
    name = "ja3_malware"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        known = config.ja3_known_malware
        if not known:
            return []

        buckets: dict[str, dict] = defaultdict(lambda: {
            "flow_count": 0,
            "src_ips": Counter(),
            "dst_ips": Counter(),
            "dst_ports": Counter(),
            "first_seen": None,
            "last_seen": None,
        })

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if not flow.ja3 or flow.ja3 not in known:
                continue
            b = buckets[flow.ja3]
            b["flow_count"] += 1
            b["src_ips"][key.src_ip] += 1
            b["dst_ips"][key.dst_ip] += 1
            if key.dst_port is not None:
                b["dst_ports"][key.dst_port] += 1
            if flow.first_ts is not None:
                if b["first_seen"] is None or flow.first_ts < b["first_seen"]:
                    b["first_seen"] = flow.first_ts
            if flow.last_ts is not None:
                if b["last_seen"] is None or flow.last_ts > b["last_seen"]:
                    b["last_seen"] = flow.last_ts

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for ja3, b in buckets.items():
            name = known[ja3]
            top_srcs = [
                {"ip": ip, "count": c}
                for ip, c in b["src_ips"].most_common(5)
            ]
            top_dsts = [
                {"ip": ip, "count": c}
                for ip, c in b["dst_ips"].most_common(5)
            ]
            findings.append(Finding(
                type="ja3_malware",
                value=f"{name} (ja3={ja3[:8]}…)",
                confidence=0.85,
                evidence={
                    "ja3": ja3,
                    "tool_name": name,
                    "flow_count": b["flow_count"],
                    "top_src_ips": top_srcs,
                    "top_dst_ips": top_dsts,
                    "dst_ports": sorted(b["dst_ports"].keys()),
                },
                first_seen=b["first_seen"],
                last_seen=b["last_seen"],
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/large_transfer.py
# ==========================================
"""
LargeTransferDetector — аномально большой объём в одном flow (паре).

Сигнал: один FlowKey (или пара направлений) передал > large_transfer_min_bytes.

Что важно для триажа:
  - не «просто много байт», а КУДА много: outbound с перевесом вверх —
    эксфильтрация (T1041), inbound — выкачка payload'а/обновления (T1105).
  - internal ↔ internal (бэкап, миграция) — отдельная история, не C2.

Агрегация: пара (ip, port) + (ip, port), оба направления в один bucket.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding, FlowKey
from paradox.net_utils import is_internal_ip


# Если одна сторона >N× другой — считаем обмен односторонним.
# Порог «на глаз»: symmetric TLS handshake + data обычно дают ≥1:10.
_ASYMMETRY_RATIO = 5.0


def _pair_key(key: FlowKey) -> tuple:
    """
    Нормализованный ключ пары: (ip_a, port_a, ip_b, port_b, proto).
    Прямой и обратный flow дают одинаковый ключ.
    """
    a = (key.src_ip, key.src_port or 0)
    b = (key.dst_ip, key.dst_port or 0)
    if a <= b:
        return (a, b, key.proto)
    return (b, a, key.proto)


def _direction_for(src_ip: str, dst_ip: str, config: Config) -> str:
    """
    inbound  — внешний → внутренний
    outbound — внутренний → внешний
    internal — внутренний ↔ внутренний
    external — внешний ↔ внешний (редкость в enterprise pcap)
    """
    src_in = is_internal_ip(src_ip, config)
    dst_in = is_internal_ip(dst_ip, config)
    if src_in and dst_in:
        return "internal"
    if src_in:
        return "outbound"
    if dst_in:
        return "inbound"
    return "external"


def _mitre_hint_for(
    direction: str, bytes_in: int, bytes_out: int,
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """
    MITRE-подсказка по направлению + асимметрии.

    - outbound с большим upload → T1041 (Exfil Over C2 Channel)
    - inbound с большим download → T1105 (Ingress Tool Transfer)
    - internal ↔ internal → T1071.001 (Application Layer Protocol)
    - симметричный внешний обмен — без техники (T1071.001 слишком общий).
    """
    if direction == "outbound" and bytes_out > bytes_in * _ASYMMETRY_RATIO:
        return ("T1041",)
    if direction == "inbound" and bytes_in > bytes_out * _ASYMMETRY_RATIO:
        return ("T1105",)
    if direction == "internal":
        return ("T1071.001",)
    return ()


class LargeTransferDetector:
    name = "large_transfer"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        threshold = config.large_transfer_min_bytes
        include_reverse = config.large_transfer_include_reverse

        if include_reverse:
            findings = self._run_pair_mode(state, config, threshold)
        else:
            findings = self._run_single_mode(state, config, threshold)

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: -f.evidence["total_bytes"])
        return findings

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # single mode — один направленный flow
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------

    def _run_single_mode(
            self, state: CaptureState, config: Config, threshold: int,
    ) -> list[Finding]:
        excluded_hosts = config.large_transfer_excluded_hosts
        excluded_uas = config.large_transfer_excluded_user_agents

        def _is_excluded(flow) -> bool:
            host = (flow.http_host or "").lower()
            ua = (flow.http_user_agent or "").lower()
            if any(h in host for h in excluded_hosts):
                return True
            if any(u.lower() in ua for u in excluded_uas):
                return True
            return False

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if _is_excluded(flow):
                continue
            if flow.bytes_sent < threshold:
                continue

            direction = _direction_for(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, config)
            # bytes_in/out относительно internal-стороны.
            # В single mode видим только одну сторону потока.
            if direction == "outbound":
                bytes_out, bytes_in = flow.bytes_sent, 0
            elif direction == "inbound":
                bytes_out, bytes_in = 0, flow.bytes_sent
            else:
                # internal / external: понятия in/out размыты,
                # кладём всё в out — важно только направление direction.
                bytes_out, bytes_in = flow.bytes_sent, 0

            mitre = _mitre_hint_for(direction, bytes_in, bytes_out)

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="large_transfer",
                value=f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}:{key.dst_port}",
                confidence=0.85,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": key.src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": key.dst_ip,
                    "src_port": key.src_port,
                    "dst_port": key.dst_port,
                    "proto": key.proto,
                    "total_bytes": flow.bytes_sent,
                    "bytes_in": bytes_in,
                    "bytes_out": bytes_out,
                    "packets": flow.packets,
                    "direction": direction,
                    "sni": flow.sni,
                },
                first_seen=flow.first_ts,
                last_seen=flow.last_ts,
                mitre_techniques=mitre,
            ))
        return findings

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # pair mode — сумма по обоим направлениям
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------

    def _run_pair_mode(
            self, state: CaptureState, config: Config, threshold: int,
    ) -> list[Finding]:
        excluded_hosts = config.large_transfer_excluded_hosts
        excluded_uas = config.large_transfer_excluded_user_agents

        def _is_excluded(flow) -> bool:
            host = (flow.http_host or "").lower()
            ua = (flow.http_user_agent or "").lower()
            if any(h in host for h in excluded_hosts):
                return True
            if any(u.lower() in ua for u in excluded_uas):
                return True
            return False

        buckets: dict[tuple, dict] = {}

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if _is_excluded(flow):
                continue
            pair = _pair_key(key)
            b = buckets.get(pair)
            if b is None:
                # Фиксируем A-сторону = src первого увиденного flow.
                # Порядок в state.flows недетерминирован между запусками,
                # но внутри одного bucket всё согласовано.
                b = {
                    "ip_a": key.src_ip, "port_a": key.src_port,
                    "ip_b": key.dst_ip, "port_b": key.dst_port,
                    "proto": key.proto,
                    "bytes_a_to_b": 0,
                    "bytes_b_to_a": 0,
                    "total_packets": 0,
                    "flows": 0,
                    "sni": None,
                    "first_seen": None,
                    "last_seen": None,
                }
                buckets[pair] = b

            if key.src_ip == b["ip_a"] and key.dst_ip == b["ip_b"]:
                b["bytes_a_to_b"] += flow.bytes_sent
            else:
                b["bytes_b_to_a"] += flow.bytes_sent

            b["total_packets"] += flow.packets
            b["flows"] += 1
            if flow.sni and not b["sni"]:
                b["sni"] = flow.sni
            if flow.first_ts is not None:
                if b["first_seen"] is None or flow.first_ts < b["first_seen"]:
                    b["first_seen"] = flow.first_ts
            if flow.last_ts is not None:
                if b["last_seen"] is None or flow.last_ts > b["last_seen"]:
                    b["last_seen"] = flow.last_ts

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for b in buckets.values():
            total = b["bytes_a_to_b"] + b["bytes_b_to_a"]
            if total < threshold:
                continue

            direction = _direction_for(b["ip_a"], b["ip_b"], config)

            # Приводим bytes_in/out к internal-перспективе.
            if direction == "inbound":
                # ip_a — внешний, ip_b — internal: a→b это inbound
                bytes_in = b["bytes_a_to_b"]
                bytes_out = b["bytes_b_to_a"]
            else:
                # outbound / internal / external: считаем a→b за out
                bytes_out = b["bytes_a_to_b"]
                bytes_in = b["bytes_b_to_a"]

            mitre = _mitre_hint_for(direction, bytes_in, bytes_out)

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="large_transfer",
                value=f"{b['ip_a']} <-> {b['ip_b']}:{b['port_b']}",
                confidence=0.85,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": b["ip_a"],
                    "dst_ip": b["ip_b"],
                    "src_port": b["port_a"],
                    "dst_port": b["port_b"],
                    "proto": b["proto"],
                    "total_bytes": total,
                    "bytes_in": bytes_in,
                    "bytes_out": bytes_out,
                    "total_packets": b["total_packets"],
                    "flow_count": b["flows"],
                    "direction": direction,
                    "sni": b["sni"],
                },
                first_seen=b["first_seen"],
                last_seen=b["last_seen"],
                mitre_techniques=mitre,
            ))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/detectors/lateral_movement.py
# ==========================================
"""
LateralMovementDetector — признаки перемещения по внутренней сети.

Сигнал: внутренний → внутренний трафик на порты удалённого управления
(445 SMB, 135 RPC, 139 NetBIOS, 3389 RDP, 5985/5986 WinRM) ИЛИ
HTTP-запросы к /wsman, /cimv2 ИЛИ User-Agent инструментов
(WinRM, PsExec, Impacket).

Confidence:
  - 0.8 — если сработал UA-маркер или URI-маркер
  - 0.6 — если только порт, и только при >= N flows к одной паре
          (один RDP-сеанс к jump-host — норма, много flows —
          перебор/скан/латералка)

allowlist: config.lateral_movement_admin_srcs — jump-хосты, которые
ходят на эти порты как часть нормальной работы. Пусто по умолчанию.

Агрегация по (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port). Несколько flows к одному
хосту — один finding.
"""
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    flow_count: int = 0
    user_agents: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    uris: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    match_reasons: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None

    def touch(self, ts: float | None) -> None:
        if ts is None:
            return
        if self.first_seen is None or ts < self.first_seen:
            self.first_seen = ts
        if self.last_seen is None or ts > self.last_seen:
            self.last_seen = ts


def _match_ua(ua: str | None, markers: frozenset[str]) -> str | None:
    if not ua:
        return None
    ua_lc = ua.lower()
    for m in markers:
        if m.lower() in ua_lc:
            return m
    return None


def _match_uri(uri: str | None, markers: frozenset[str]) -> str | None:
    if not uri:
        return None
    uri_lc = uri.lower()
    for m in markers:
        if m.lower() in uri_lc:
            return m
    return None


class LateralMovementDetector:
    name = "lateral_movement"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        ports = config.lateral_movement_ports
        ua_markers = config.lateral_movement_ua_markers
        uri_markers = config.lateral_movement_uri_markers
        admin_srcs = config.lateral_movement_admin_srcs

        agg: dict[tuple[str, str, int], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            if key.dst_port not in ports:
                continue
            # src должен быть внутренним. Иначе — обычный трафик
            # из интернета на наш SMB-порт, это другой сигнал
            # (см. suspicious_port / port_scan).
            if not self._is_internal(key.src_ip, config):
                continue
            # allowlist: IP, которые легитимно ходят на эти порты
            if key.src_ip in admin_srcs:
                continue

            bucket = agg[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, key.dst_port)]
            bucket.flow_count += 1

            ua_hit = _match_ua(flow.http_user_agent, ua_markers)
            if ua_hit:
                bucket.user_agents.add(flow.http_user_agent)
                bucket.match_reasons.add("user_agent")

            uri_hit = _match_uri(flow.http_uri, uri_markers)
            if uri_hit:
                bucket.uris.add(flow.http_uri)
                bucket.match_reasons.add("uri")

            bucket.touch(flow.first_ts)
            bucket.touch(flow.last_ts)

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port), bucket in agg.items():
            # Порт сам по себе флагуется только при множестве flows.
            # Один RDP-сеанс к jump-host — это норма, не finding.
            if not bucket.match_reasons:
                if bucket.flow_count < config.lateral_movement_min_flows_port_only:
                    continue
                bucket.match_reasons.add("port")

            if ("user_agent" in bucket.match_reasons
                    or "uri" in bucket.match_reasons):
                confidence = 0.8
            else:
                confidence = 0.6

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="lateral_movement",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:{dst_port}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "flow_count": bucket.flow_count,
                    "match_reasons": sorted(bucket.match_reasons),
                    "user_agents": sorted(bucket.user_agents)[:5],
                    "uris": sorted(bucket.uris)[:5],
                },
                first_seen=bucket.first_seen,
                last_seen=bucket.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings

    def _is_internal(self, ip: str, config: Config) -> bool:
        return any(ip.startswith(p) for p in config.private_networks)


# ==========================================
