    if ioc["domains"]:
        lines.append("# Domains")
        for d in ioc["domains"]:
            lines.append(d["value"])
        lines.append("")
    if ioc["ips"]:
        lines.append("# IPs")
        for i in ioc["ips"]:
            lines.append(i["value"])
        lines.append("")
    if ioc["urls"]:
        lines.append("# URLs")
        for u in ioc["urls"]:
            lines.append(u["value"])
        lines.append("")
    if ioc["sha256"]:
        lines.append("# SHA256")
        for s in ioc["sha256"]:
            lines.append(s["value"])
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_iocs_csv(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """CSV: type,value,severity,source."""
    buf = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(buf)
    w.writerow(["type", "value", "severity", "source"])
    for d in ioc["domains"]:
        w.writerow(["domain", d["value"],
                    d.get("severity", ""), d.get("source", "")])
    for i in ioc["ips"]:
        w.writerow(["ip", i["value"],
                    i.get("severity", ""), i.get("source", "")])
    for u in ioc["urls"]:
        w.writerow(["url", u["value"],
                    u.get("severity", ""), u.get("source", "")])
    for s in ioc["sha256"]:
        w.writerow(["sha256", s["value"], "", s.get("source", "")])
    return buf.getvalue()


def render_iocs_json(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """Структурированный JSON."""
    return json.dumps(ioc, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)


def render_suricata_rules(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """
    Suricata-правила для домен/IP с severity >= high.

    SID с базой 9000000 — не пересекается с публичными правилами.
    """
    lines: list[str] = [
        "# Paradox generated rules",
        "# SID base 9000000 — local use",
        "",
    ]
    sid = 9000000

    for d in ioc["domains"]:
        if not _sev_ge(d.get("severity", "medium"), "high"):
            continue
        sid += 1
        val = d["value"]
        lines.append(
            f'alert dns $HOME_NET any -> any any '
            f'(msg:"Paradox: malicious domain {val}"; '
            f'dns_query; content:"{val}"; nocase; '
            f'sid:{sid}; rev:1;)'
        )

    for i in ioc["ips"]:
        if not _sev_ge(i.get("severity", "medium"), "high"):
            continue
        sid += 1
        val = i["value"]
        lines.append(
            f'alert ip $HOME_NET any -> {val} any '
            f'(msg:"Paradox: suspicious IP {val}"; '
            f'sid:{sid}; rev:1;)'
        )

    lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_queries(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """
    KQL (Sentinel/Defender) + Splunk-запросы.

    Аналитик копирует в EDR/SIEM, получает совпадения.
    """
    parts: list[str] = []

    doms = [d["value"] for d in ioc["domains"]]
    ips = [i["value"] for i in ioc["ips"]]
    shas = [s["value"] for s in ioc["sha256"]]

    parts.append("# KQL — Microsoft Sentinel / Defender")
    parts.append("")
    if doms:
        doms_list = ", ".join(f'"{d}"' for d in doms)
        parts.append("# Domains")
        parts.append("DeviceNetworkEvents")
        parts.append(f"| where RemoteUrl in ({doms_list})")
        parts.append("")
    if ips:
        ips_list = ", ".join(f'"{i}"' for i in ips)
        parts.append("# IPs")
        parts.append("DeviceNetworkEvents")
        parts.append(f"| where RemoteIP in ({ips_list})")
        parts.append("")
    if shas:
        shas_list = ", ".join(f'"{s}"' for s in shas)
        parts.append("# SHA256 — поиск запуска файлов")
        parts.append("DeviceProcessEvents")
        parts.append(f"| where SHA256 in ({shas_list})")
        parts.append("")

    parts.append("# Splunk SPL")
    parts.append("")
    if doms:
        doms_or = " OR ".join(f'query="{d}"' for d in doms)
        parts.append("# Domains")
        parts.append(f'index=* ({doms_or})')
        parts.append("")
    if ips:
        ips_or = " OR ".join(f'dest="{i}"' for i in ips)
        parts.append("# IPs")
        parts.append(f'index=* ({ips_or})')
        parts.append("")
    if shas:
        shas_or = " OR ".join(f'sha256="{s}"' for s in shas)
        parts.append("# SHA256")
        parts.append(f'index=* ({shas_or})')
        parts.append("")

    return "\n".join(parts)


def write_ioc_files(ioc: dict, output_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """
    Записать пять файлов в output_dir.

    Возвращает {формат: путь}. Если пусто — пустой dict.
    """
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    has_any = any(ioc[k] for k in ("domains", "ips", "sha256", "urls"))
    if not has_any:
        return {}

    written: dict[str, Path] = {}
    mapping = {
        "txt": ("iocs.txt", render_iocs_txt),
        "csv": ("iocs.csv", render_iocs_csv),
        "json": ("iocs.json", render_iocs_json),
        "suricata": ("suricata.rules", render_suricata_rules),
        "queries": ("queries.txt", render_queries),
    }
    for fmt, (name, renderer) in mapping.items():
        content = renderer(ioc)
        if not content.strip():
            continue
        p = output_dir / name
        p.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
        written[fmt] = p
    return written


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/reporters/json_reporter.py
# ==========================================
import json
from pathlib import Path


def write_json(report: dict, path: Path) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)


def write_markdown(report: dict, path) -> None:
    """Записать Markdown-версию отчёта."""
    from paradox.reporters.markdown_reporter import render_markdown
    from pathlib import Path

    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(render_markdown(report))


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/reporters/markdown_reporter.py
# ==========================================
"""
Markdown-рендер отчёта.

Берёт dict от run_pipeline, возвращает строку в Markdown.
Цель — то, что аналитик копирует в тикет или вики.

Принцип: то же самое, что в JSON, но человекочитаемо.
Не парсим JSON обратно — работаем с dict напрямую.

Структура отчёта (0.7.0):
1. Header (мета)
2. TL;DR — что произошло, кто атакует, что скачано (в порядке kill-chain)
3. Timeline — хронология findings
4. Carved Files — метаданные payload'ов
5. Сводка — числа
6. Сущности — внутренние, внешние (с ролью pivot)
7. Findings — с MITRE-маппингом
8. Противоречия — несогласованности между источниками
9. HTTP — хосты, URI, UA
10. IOC — домены, IP, порты
11. Топ-10 flows
"""
from __future__ import annotations

# v0.8.0: timeline

from ipaddress import ip_address


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# MITRE ATT&CK маппинг: тип finding → ID + тактика
# Источник: https://attack.mitre.org/
#
# ВАЖНО: это fallback. Если у Finding есть instance-level
# mitre_techniques (заполнены детектором — например large_transfer
# по direction), используем их, а не этот словарь.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
_MITRE_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    "brute_force":        "T1110 (Brute Force)",
    "ftp_brute_force":    "T1110.001 (Password Guessing)",
    "command_injection":  "T1059 (Command & Scripting Interpreter)",
    "script_download":    "T1105 (Ingress Tool Transfer)",
    "lateral_movement":   "T1021 (Remote Services)",
    "large_transfer":     "T1041 (Exfil Over C2 Channel)",
    "beaconing":          "T1071 (Application Layer Protocol)",
    "dns_tunneling":      "T1071.004 (DNS)",
    "dga_domain":         "T1568.002 (Domain Generation Algorithms)",
    "typosquatting":      "T1583.001 (Acquire Infrastructure: Domains)",
    "vulnerable_path":    "T1190 (Exploit Public-Facing App)",
    "web_scanner":        "T1595.002 (Vulnerability Scanning)",
    "port_scan":          "T1046 (Network Service Discovery)",
    "sni_without_dns":    "T1071.001 (Web Protocols)",
    "ja3_malware":        "T1071.001 (Web Protocols)",
    "ja3_tool":           "—",
    "cloud_tunnel":       "T1572 (Protocol Tunneling)",
    "ip_check_service":   "T1590.005 (IP Addresses)",
    "nxdomain_burst":     "T1568 (Dynamic Resolution)",
    "suspicious_port":    "T1571 (Non-Standard Port)",
    "carved_file":        "T1105 (Ingress Tool Transfer)",
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Kill-chain ordering
# Порядок отражает типичную последовательность атаки.
# Не найденные типы не влияют; незнакомые типы уезжают в конец.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
_CHAIN_ORDER: dict[str, int] = {
    "pivot":              5,   # pivot — уже компромисс, показываем первым
    "web_scanner":       10,
    "port_scan":         20,
    "vulnerable_path":   30,
    "command_injection": 40,
    "brute_force":       50,
    "ftp_brute_force":   55,
    "script_download":   60,
    "carved_file":       65,
    "suspicious_port":   70,
    "ja3_malware":       75,
    "ja3_tool":          76,
    "beaconing":         80,
    "dns_tunneling":     82,
    "dga_domain":        84,
    "nxdomain_burst":    86,
    "typosquatting":     88,
    "cloud_tunnel":      90,
    "sni_without_dns":   92,
    "ip_check_service":  94,
    "lateral_movement": 100,
    "large_transfer":   110,
}
_CHAIN_UNKNOWN = 999


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Форматтеры
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _fmt_bytes(n: int) -> str:
    """Человекочитаемый размер."""
    for unit in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"):
        if n < 1024:
            return f"{n:.1f} {unit}" if unit != "B" else f"{n} {unit}"
        n /= 1024
    return f"{n:.1f} PB"


def _fmt_duration(seconds: float) -> str:
    """Человекочитаемая длительность."""
    if seconds < 60:
        return f"{seconds:.1f} сек"
    if seconds < 3600:
        return f"{seconds / 60:.1f} мин"
    if seconds < 86400:
        return f"{seconds / 3600:.1f} ч"
    return f"{seconds / 86400:.1f} дн"


def _fmt_rel(seconds: float) -> str:
    """Время в формате H:MM:SS для timeline."""
    if seconds < 0:
        seconds = 0
    h = int(seconds // 3600)
    m = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)
    s = int(seconds % 60)
    if h > 0:
        return f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
    return f"{m}:{s:02d}"


def _short(s: str, n: int = 40) -> str:
    """Обрезать длинную строку с многоточием."""
    if not s:
        return ""
    return s if len(s) <= n else s[: n - 1] + "…"


def _fmt_host(host: str | None) -> str:
    """
    Host из HTTP-таблицы. Если это IP-литерал — помечаем как unknown host.
    Иначе — обычное имя.
    """
    if not host:
        return "?"
    try:
        ip_address(host)
    except ValueError:
        return host
    return f"{host} (unknown host)"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# MITRE
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _mitre_for_finding(f: dict) -> str:
    """
    Instance-level mitre_techniques приоритетнее type-map.

    large_transfer знает про T1041/T1105 сам (по direction),
    а _MITRE_MAP знает только общий T1041.
    """
    techniques = f.get("mitre_techniques") or ()
    if techniques:
        return ", ".join(techniques)
    return _MITRE_MAP.get(f.get("type", ""), "—")


# Совместимость: старое имя, использовалось в timeline/findings.
def _mitre_for(finding_type: str) -> str:
    return _MITRE_MAP.get(finding_type, "—")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# IOC extraction
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _extract_iocs(findings: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Простое извлечение IOC из findings."""
    domains: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    ips: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    ports: dict[int, list[str]] = {}

    for f in findings:
        ftype = f.get("type", "")
        value = f.get("value", "")

        if ftype in ("dga_domain", "cloud_tunnel_domain",
                     "ip_check_service", "typosquatting",
                     "sni_without_dns"):
            domains.setdefault(value, []).append(ftype)

        elif ftype == "suspicious_port":
            if ":" in value:
                ip, port_s = value.rsplit(":", 1)
                ips.setdefault(ip, []).append(ftype)
                try:
                    ports.setdefault(int(port_s), []).append(ftype)
                except ValueError:
                    pass

        elif ftype == "nxdomain_burst":
            ips.setdefault(value, []).append(ftype)

    return {"domains": domains, "ips": ips, "ports": ports}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# TL;DR
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _pick_first_with_role(entities: list[dict], role: str) -> str | None:
    for e in entities:
        if role in e.get("roles", []):
            return e.get("ip")
    return None


def _unique_finding_types(findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    seen: list[str] = []
    for f in findings:
        t = f.get("type")
        if t and t not in seen:
            seen.append(t)
    return seen


def _chain_for(findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """
    Уникальные типы findings в порядке kill-chain.

    Приоритет — цепочка, а не confidence: аналитик должен видеть
    «скан → RCE → payload → beacon», даже если beacon confidence выше.
    """
    seen: set[str] = set()
    ordered: list[str] = []
    for f in findings:
        t = f.get("type")
        if t and t not in seen:
            seen.add(t)
            ordered.append(t)
    return sorted(
        ordered,
        key=lambda t: (_CHAIN_ORDER.get(t, _CHAIN_UNKNOWN), t),
    )


def _render_tldr(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    """Короткий TL;DR: что произошло, кто атакует, что скачано."""
    findings = report.get("findings", [])
    entities = report.get("entities", {})
    internal = entities.get("internal", [])
    carved = report.get("carved_files", {}).get("files", [])
    contradictions = report.get("contradictions") or []

    lines: list[str] = ["## TL;DR", ""]

    if not findings and not contradictions:
        lines.append("_Findings нет. Трафик выглядит чистым на уровне эвристик._")
        lines.append("")
        return lines

    attacker = _pick_first_with_role(internal, "attacker_candidate")
    victim = _pick_first_with_role(internal, "victim_candidate")
    pivot = _pick_first_with_role(internal, "pivot")

    # Основное предложение — цепочка в порядке kill-chain.
    types = _chain_for(findings)
    types_str = " → ".join(f"`{t}`" for t in types) if types else "—"

    if pivot:
        lines.append(
            f"**PIVOT:** `{pivot}` — скомпрометирован и атакует дальше. "
            f"Типы: {types_str}."
        )
    elif attacker and victim:
        lines.append(
            f"**Инцидент:** `{attacker}` атакует `{victim}`. "
            f"Типы: {types_str}."
        )
    elif victim:
        lines.append(f"**Жертва:** `{victim}`. Типы: {types_str}.")
    elif attacker:
        lines.append(f"**Атакующий:** `{attacker}`. Типы: {types_str}.")
    else:
        lines.append(f"**Findings:** {types_str}.")
    lines.append("")

    # Ключевое — список
    if pivot:
        lines.append(f"- **Pivot:** `{pivot}` (внутренний, критично)")
    if attacker:
        lines.append(f"- Attacker: `{attacker}` (internal)")
    if victim:
        lines.append(f"- Victim: `{victim}` (internal)")

    # Payloads
    for f in carved[:3]:
        uri = f.get("uri", "?")
        size = f.get("size", 0)
        sha = (f.get("sha256") or "")[:16]
        ext = f.get("ext") or "?"
        lines.append(
            f"- Payload: `{uri}` ({ext}, {size} B, sha256 `{sha}…`)"
        )
    if len(carved) > 3:
        lines.append(f"- _(ещё {len(carved) - 3} payload'ов — см. Carved Files)_")

    # High-confidence findings
    high = [f for f in findings if f.get("confidence", 0) >= 0.85]
    if high:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(f"**High-confidence ({len(high)}):**")
        for f in high[:5]:
            ftype = f.get("type", "?")
            value = _short(f.get("value", "?"), 70)
            lines.append(f"- `{ftype}` — `{value}` ({f.get('confidence', 0):.2f})")

    # Contradictions, если есть — короткая ссылка на секцию.
    if contradictions:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(
            f"**Противоречия:** {len(contradictions)} — см. секцию ниже."
        )

    lines.append("")
    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Timeline
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _escape_cell(s: str) -> str:
    """Экранировать | для markdown-таблицы."""
    return (s or "").replace("|", "\\|")


def _render_timeline(report: dict, top_n: int = 50) -> list[str]:
    """
    Хронология findings + contradictions по фазам kill-chain.

    v0.8.0: читаем report["timeline"] — плоский список событий,
    отсортированный по ts. Группируем по phase (kill-chain), внутри
    фазы — таблица событий. Fallback на старое поведение, если
    timeline в отчёте отсутствует.
    """
    timeline = report.get("timeline")
    if not timeline:
        return _render_timeline_fallback(report, top_n=15)

    lines: list[str] = ["## Timeline", ""]
    lines.append(
        "_Хронология findings и противоречий по фазам kill-chain. "
        "Время — относительно первого события._"
    )
    lines.append("")

    # t0 — минимум ts среди всех событий.
    t0 = min(e.get("ts", 0.0) for e in timeline)

    # Группировка по phase, порядок фаз — по chain_order.
    by_phase: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    phase_order: dict[str, int] = {}
    for e in timeline:
        phase = e.get("phase", "other")
        by_phase.setdefault(phase, []).append(e)
        phase_order[phase] = e.get("chain_order", 999)

    ordered_phases = sorted(
        by_phase.keys(),
        key=lambda p: (phase_order.get(p, 999), p),
    )

    shown = 0
    for phase in ordered_phases:
        events = by_phase[phase]
        lines.append(f"### {phase} ({len(events)})")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(
            "| Время (rel) | Kind | Тип | Subject | Описание | Conf | MITRE |"
        )
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
        for e in events:
            if shown >= top_n:
                break
            rel = _fmt_rel(e.get("ts", 0.0) - t0)
            kind = e.get("kind", "?")
            etype = _escape_cell(e.get("type", "?"))
            subject = _escape_cell(_short(e.get("subject") or "?", 20))
            desc = _escape_cell(_short(e.get("description") or "", 70))
            conf = e.get("confidence", 0.0)
            mitre = e.get("mitre_id") or "—"
            lines.append(
                f"| {rel} | `{kind}` | `{etype}` | `{subject}` | "
                f"{desc} | {conf:.2f} | {mitre} |"
            )
            shown += 1
        lines.append("")
        if shown >= top_n:
            remaining = len(timeline) - shown
            if remaining > 0:
                lines.append(
                    f"_…и ещё {remaining} событий — см. JSON-отчёт._"
                )
                lines.append("")
            break

    return lines


def _render_timeline_fallback(report: dict, top_n: int = 15) -> list[str]:
    """
    Fallback для отчётов без report["timeline"] (до v0.8.0).

    Плоская хронология findings по first_seen — как было раньше.
    """
    findings = report.get("findings", [])
    lines: list[str] = ["## Timeline", ""]

    with_ts = [f for f in findings if f.get("first_seen") is not None]
    if not with_ts:
        lines.append("_Нет timestamps у findings._")
        lines.append("")
        return lines

    t0 = min(f["first_seen"] for f in with_ts)
    sorted_f = sorted(with_ts, key=lambda f: f["first_seen"])

    lines.append("| Время (rel) | Тип | Значение | Conf | MITRE |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for f in sorted_f[:top_n]:
        rel = _fmt_rel(f["first_seen"] - t0)
        ftype = f.get("type", "?")
        value = _short(f.get("value", "?"), 60)
        conf = f.get("confidence", 0.0)
        mitre = _mitre_for_finding(f)
        lines.append(
            f"| {rel} | `{ftype}` | {value} | {conf:.2f} | {mitre} |"
        )
    if len(sorted_f) > top_n:
        lines.append(f"| ... | _(ещё {len(sorted_f) - top_n})_ | | | |")
    lines.append("")
    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Carved files (опционально — если есть)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_carved_files(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    carved = report.get("carved_files", {}).get("files", [])
    if not carved:
        return []

    lines: list[str] = [f"## Carved Files ({len(carved)})", ""]
    lines.append("_Metadata-only. Тело на диск не пишется._")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("| URI | Ext | Size | SHA256 | First bytes (hex) |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for f in carved:
        uri = _short(f.get("uri", "?"), 50)
        ext = f.get("ext") or "?"
        size = f.get("size", 0)
        sha = (f.get("sha256") or "")[:16]
        fb = _short(f.get("first_bytes_hex", "") or "", 32)
        lines.append(
            f"| `{uri}` | `{ext}` | {size} B | `{sha}…` | `{fb}` |"
        )
    lines.append("")
    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Contradictions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

_SEVERITY_ICON: dict[str, str] = {
    "critical": "🔴",
    "high": "🟠",
    "medium": "🟡",
    "low": "🟢",
    "info": "⚪",
}


def _render_contradictions(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    contradictions = report.get("contradictions") or []
    if not contradictions:
        return []

    lines: list[str] = [f"## Противоречия ({len(contradictions)})", ""]
    lines.append(
        "_Несогласованности между источниками. Не атаки, а сигналы "
        "«одно из двух неверно, разберись»._"
    )
    lines.append("")

    for c in contradictions:
        sev = c.get("severity", "info")
        icon = _SEVERITY_ICON.get(sev, "·")
        rule = c.get("rule", "?")
        subject = c.get("subject", "?")
        conf = c.get("confidence", 0.0)

        lines.append(f"### {icon} `{rule}` — `{subject}` ({conf:.2f})")
        lines.append("")

        hint = c.get("resolution_hint")
        if hint:
            lines.append(f"> {hint}")
            lines.append("")

        lines.append("**Факты:**")
        lines.append("")
        for f in c.get("facts", []):
            src = f.get("source", "?")
            pred = f.get("predicate", "?")
            val = f.get("value") or "(unknown)"
            ts = f.get("ts", 0.0)
            fc = f.get("confidence", 0.0)
            # finding_type из evidence — отличает nxdomain_burst от port_scan
            # в отчёте, когда value пуст.
            ftype = (f.get("evidence") or {}).get("finding_type") or ""
            ftype_str = f" [{ftype}]" if ftype else ""
            lines.append(
                f"- `{pred}` ← `{val}`{ftype_str} "
                f"(source=`{src}`, ts={ts:.1f}, conf={fc:.2f})"
            )
        lines.append("")

        causes = c.get("likely_causes") or []
        if causes:
            lines.append("**Вероятные причины:**")
            lines.append("")
            for cause in causes:
                lines.append(f"- {cause}")
            lines.append("")

    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main render
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def render_markdown(report: dict) -> str:
    """Собрать Markdown-отчёт из dict run_pipeline."""
    lines: list[str] = []

    provenance = report.get("provenance", {})
    summary = report.get("summary", {})
    findings = report.get("findings", [])
    flows = report.get("flows", {})
    entities = report.get("entities", {})
    http = report.get("http", {})
    top_flows = flows.get("top_by_bytes", [])
    contradictions = report.get("contradictions") or []

    # --- header ---
    pcap_path = provenance.get("pcap_path", "?")
    pcap_name = pcap_path.split("\\")[-1].split("/")[-1]
    lines.append(f"# PCAP Triage Report: `{pcap_name}`")
    lines.append("")

    lines.append(f"**Инструмент:** Paradox v{provenance.get('tool_version', '?')}  ")
    lines.append(f"**Файл:** `{pcap_path}`  ")
    lines.append(f"**SHA256:** `{provenance.get('pcap_sha256', '?')}`  ")
    lines.append("")

    # --- TL;DR ---
    lines.extend(_render_tldr(report))

    # --- Absence ---
    lines.extend(_render_absence(report))

    # --- Timeline ---
    if findings:
        lines.extend(_render_timeline(report))

    # --- Carved files ---
    carved_section = _render_carved_files(report)
    if carved_section:
        lines.extend(carved_section)

    # --- summary ---
    lines.append("## Сводка")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append(f"- **Пакеты:** {summary.get('packets', 0):,}")
    lines.append(f"- **Flows:** {summary.get('flows', 0):,}")
    lines.append(f"- **Уникальных DNS-запросов:** {summary.get('unique_dns_queries', 0)}")
    lines.append(f"- **NXDOMAIN:** {summary.get('nxdomain_count', 0)}")
    lines.append(f"- **Findings:** {summary.get('findings', 0)}")
    if contradictions:
        lines.append(f"- **Противоречий:** {len(contradictions)}")

    first_ts = summary.get("first_ts")
    last_ts = summary.get("last_ts")
    if first_ts and last_ts:
        duration = last_ts - first_ts
        lines.append(f"- **Период:** {_fmt_duration(duration)}")

    uploaded = summary.get("uploaded_bytes")
    downloaded = summary.get("downloaded_bytes")
    if uploaded is not None and downloaded is not None:
        lines.append(f"- **Uploaded (internal → external):** {_fmt_bytes(uploaded)}")
        lines.append(f"- **Downloaded (external → internal):** {_fmt_bytes(downloaded)}")

    extractor_errors = summary.get("extractor_errors") or {}
    if extractor_errors:
        lines.append(f"- **Ошибки экстракторов:** `{extractor_errors}`")
    lines.append("")

    # --- entities ---
    if entities:
        lines.extend(_render_entities(entities, findings))
        lines.append("")

    # --- NDP ---
    lines.extend(_render_ndp(report))

    # --- findings ---
    lines.extend(_render_findings(findings))

    # --- contradictions ---
    contradictions_section = _render_contradictions(report)
    if contradictions_section:
        lines.extend(contradictions_section)
        lines.append("")

    # --- HTTP ---
    if http:
        lines.extend(_render_http(http))
        lines.append("")

    # --- IOC ---
    iocs = _extract_iocs(findings)
    if iocs["domains"] or iocs["ips"] or iocs["ports"]:
        lines.append("## IOC")
        lines.append("")

        if iocs["domains"]:
            lines.append("**Домены:**")
            lines.append("")
            for domain, reasons in sorted(iocs["domains"].items()):
                lines.append(f"- `{domain}` — {', '.join(reasons)}")
            lines.append("")

        if iocs["ips"]:
            lines.append("**IP:**")
            lines.append("")
            for ip, reasons in sorted(iocs["ips"].items()):
                lines.append(f"- `{ip}` — {', '.join(reasons)}")
            lines.append("")

        if iocs["ports"]:
            lines.append("**Порты:**")
            lines.append("")
            for port, reasons in sorted(iocs["ports"].items()):
                lines.append(f"- `{port}` — {', '.join(reasons)}")
            lines.append("")

    # --- top flows ---
    if top_flows:
        lines.append("## Топ-10 flows по байтам")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("| Src | Dst | Dir | Proto | Bytes | SNI |")
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
        for fl in top_flows[:10]:
            src = f"{fl.get('src_ip', '?')}:{fl.get('src_port') or '-'}"
            dst = f"{fl.get('dst_ip', '?')}:{fl.get('dst_port') or '-'}"
            direction = fl.get("direction") or "—"
            proto = fl.get("proto", "?")
            b = _fmt_bytes(fl.get("bytes", 0))
            sni = _short(fl.get("sni") or "—", 60)
            lines.append(
                f"| `{src}` | `{dst}` | {direction} | {proto} | {b} | `{sni}` |"
            )
        lines.append("")

    # --- footer ---
    lines.append("---")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("_Отчёт сгенерирован автоматически. Проверяй findings глазами: "
                 "эвристики дают кандидатов, не приговоры._")
    lines.append("")

    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Findings
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_findings(findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    lines: list[str] = []
    if not findings:
        lines.append("## Findings")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("_Ничего не найдено._")
        lines.append("")
        return lines

    lines.append(f"## Findings ({len(findings)})")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("| Тип | Значение | Conf | MITRE |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
    for f in findings[:50]:
        ftype = _escape_cell(str(f.get("type", "?")))
        value = _escape_cell(_short(str(f.get("value", "")), 60))
        conf = f.get("confidence")
        conf_str = f"{conf:.2f}" if isinstance(conf, (int, float)) else "—"
        mitre = _escape_cell(_mitre_for_finding(f))
        lines.append(f"| `{ftype}` | {value} | {conf_str} | {mitre} |")
    if len(findings) > 50:
        lines.append(f"| ... | _(ещё {len(findings) - 50})_ | | |")
    lines.append("")
    return lines

def _finding_types_for(entity: dict, findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """Преобразовать findings_indices → список типов."""
    idxs = entity.get("findings_indices", []) or []
    types: list[str] = []
    for i in idxs:
        if 0 <= i < len(findings):
            t = findings[i].get("type")
            if t and t not in types:
                types.append(t)
    return types


def _render_entities(entities: dict, findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """Секция 'Сущности' в Markdown."""
    lines: list[str] = []

    internal = entities.get("internal", [])
    external = entities.get("external", [])

    if not internal and not external:
        return []

    lines.append("## Сущности")
    lines.append("")

    if internal:
        lines.append(f"### Внутренние хосты ({len(internal)})")
        lines.append("")
        for e in internal:
            ip = e.get("ip", "?")
            roles = e.get("roles", [])
            roles_str = ", ".join(f"`{r}`" for r in roles) if roles else "—"
            lines.append(f"**`{ip}`** — {roles_str}  ")

            fo = e.get("flows_out", 0)
            fi = e.get("flows_in", 0)
            bo = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_out", 0))
            bi = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_in", 0))
            lines.append(f"  {fo} flows out · {fi} in · ↓ {bi} · ↑ {bo}  ")

            mac = e.get("mac")
            hostname = e.get("hostname")
            username = e.get("username")
            realm = e.get("realm")
            if mac or hostname or username or realm:
                parts = []
                if hostname:
                    parts.append(f"hostname: `{hostname}`")
                if mac:
                    parts.append(f"MAC: `{mac}`")
                if username:
                    parts.append(f"username: `{username}`")
                if realm:
                    parts.append(f"realm: `{realm}`")
                lines.append("  " + " · ".join(parts) + "  ")

            sni = e.get("sni_domains", [])
            if sni:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{d}`" for d in sni[:5])
                more = f" _(+{len(sni) - 5})_" if len(sni) > 5 else ""
                lines.append(f"  SNI: {shown}{more}  ")

            dns = e.get("dns_names", [])
            if dns:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{d}`" for d in dns[:10])
                more = f" _(+{len(dns) - 10})_" if len(dns) > 10 else ""
                lines.append(f"  DNS: {shown}{more}  ")

            fnd_types = _finding_types_for(e, findings)
            if fnd_types:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{t}`" for t in fnd_types)
                lines.append(f"  Findings: {shown}  ")

            lines.append("")

    external_with_findings = [e for e in external if e.get("findings_indices")]
    if external_with_findings:
        lines.append(f"### Внешние IP с findings ({len(external_with_findings)})")
        lines.append("")
        for e in external_with_findings:
            ip = e.get("ip", "?")
            roles = e.get("roles", [])
            roles_str = ", ".join(f"`{r}`" for r in roles) if roles else "—"
            lines.append(f"**`{ip}`** — {roles_str}  ")

            fi = e.get("flows_in", 0)
            fo = e.get("flows_out", 0)
            bi = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_in", 0))
            bo = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_out", 0))
            lines.append(f"  {fi} flows in · {fo} out · ↓ {bi} · ↑ {bo}  ")

            mac = e.get("mac")
            hostname = e.get("hostname")
            if mac or hostname:
                parts = []
                if hostname:
                    parts.append(f"hostname: `{hostname}`")
                if mac:
                    parts.append(f"MAC: `{mac}`")
                lines.append("  " + " · ".join(parts) + "  ")

            sni = e.get("sni_domains", [])
            if sni:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{d}`" for d in sni[:5])
                lines.append(f"  SNI: {shown}  ")

            fnd_types = _finding_types_for(e, findings)
            if fnd_types:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{t}`" for t in fnd_types)
                lines.append(f"  Findings: {shown}  ")

            lines.append("")

    external_clean = [e for e in external if not e.get("findings_indices")]
    if external_clean:
        lines.append("### Внешние IP без findings — топ-5")
        lines.append("")
        for e in external_clean[:5]:
            ip = e.get("ip", "?")
            fi = e.get("flows_in", 0)
            bi = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_in", 0))
            lines.append(f"- `{ip}` — {fi} flows in, {bi}")
        lines.append("")

    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# HTTP
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_http(http: dict) -> list[str]:
    """Секция 'HTTP' в Markdown."""
    lines: list[str] = []

    unique_hosts = http.get("unique_hosts", 0)
    unique_uris = http.get("unique_uris", 0)
    unique_ua = http.get("unique_user_agents", 0)

    if not unique_hosts and not unique_uris:
        return []

    lines.append("## HTTP")
    lines.append("")

    methods = http.get("methods", []) or []
    if methods:
        method_str = ", ".join(
            f"{m.get('method', '?')}×{m.get('count', 0)}" for m in methods
        )
        lines.append(f"**Methods:** {method_str}  ")
    lines.append(f"**Hosts:** {unique_hosts} · **URIs:** {unique_uris} · "
                 f"**User-Agents:** {unique_ua}  ")
    lines.append("")

    top_hosts = http.get("top_hosts", []) or []
    if top_hosts:
        lines.append("### Топ Hosts")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("| Count | Host |")
        lines.append("|---|---|")
        for h in top_hosts[:10]:
            host = _fmt_host(h.get("host"))
            lines.append(f"| {h.get('count', 0)} | `{host}` |")
        lines.append("")

    top_uris = http.get("top_uris", []) or []
    if top_uris:
        lines.append("### Топ URIs")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("| Method | Host | URI | Src → Dst | UA | Bytes |")
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
        for u in top_uris[:15]:
            method = u.get("method") or "?"
            host = _fmt_host(_short(u.get("host") or "?", 30))
            uri = _short(u.get("uri") or "?", 60)
            src = f"{u.get('src_ip', '?')}:?"
            dst = f"{u.get('dst_ip', '?')}:{u.get('dst_port') or '-'}"
            ua = _short(u.get("user_agent") or "", 40)
            b = _fmt_bytes(u.get("bytes", 0))
            lines.append(
                f"| `{method}` | `{host}` | `{uri}` | `{src}` → `{dst}` | {ua} | {b} |"
            )
        lines.append("")

    top_ua = http.get("top_user_agents", []) or []
    if top_ua:
        lines.append("### Топ User-Agents")
        lines.append("")
        for ua in top_ua[:10]:
            count = ua.get("count", 0)
            agent = _short(ua.get("user_agent") or "?", 80)
            lines.append(f"- ×{count} — `{agent}`")
        lines.append("")

    return lines

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Новые секции (NDP и Absence)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_absence(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    absence = report.get("absence", {})
    dns = absence.get("dns_unanswered", [])
    tcp = absence.get("tcp_unanswered", [])

    if not dns and not tcp:
        return []

    lines = ["## Аномалии / Оборванные сессии", ""]

    if dns:
        lines.append("### DNS без ответа")
        lines.append("| Домен (QNAME) | Запросов |")
        lines.append("|---|---|")
        for d in dns:
            lines.append(f"| `{d.get('qname', '?')}` | {d.get('count', 0)} |")
        lines.append("")

    if tcp:
        lines.append("### TCP без ответа (SYN)")
        lines.append("| Src IP | Dst IP | Порт | Пакеты |")
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
        for t in tcp:
            lines.append(f"| `{t.get('src_ip', '?')}` | `{t.get('dst_ip', '?')}` | {t.get('dst_port', '?')} | {t.get('packets', 0)} |")
        lines.append("")

    return lines

def _render_ndp(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    ndp = report.get("ndp", {})
    total = ndp.get("total", 0)
    neighbors = ndp.get("neighbors", [])
    routers = ndp.get("routers", [])

    if total == 0:
        return []

    lines = [f"## IPv6 / NDP ({total} пакетов)", ""]

    if routers:
        lines.append("### Роутеры (RA)")
        for r in routers:
            lines.append(f"- `{r}`")
        lines.append("")

    if neighbors:
        lines.append("### Соседи (NDP)")
        lines.append("| IPv6 | MAC |")
        lines.append("|---|---|")
        for n in neighbors:
            lines.append(f"| `{n.get('ip', '?')}` | `{n.get('mac', '?')}` |")
        lines.append("")

    return lines



# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/timeline.py
# ==========================================
"""
Timeline / kill-chain reconstruction.

Собирает из findings и contradictions хронологию событий с
kill-chain-фазами. Нового анализа не делает — только упорядочивает
уже найденное по времени и группирует по фазам атаки.

Появился в v0.8.0.

Схема события:

    TimelineEvent:
        ts           — unix timestamp, момент события
        kind         — "finding" | "contradiction"
        type         — f.type / c.rule
        subject      — обычно IP (кто «герой» события)
        severity     — critical | high | medium | low | info
        confidence
        phase        — kill-chain фаза
        chain_order  — число для сортировки фаз (10, 20, 30, ...)
        description  — краткое человекочитаемое
        mitre_id     — T-номер (для findings из mitre_map)
        ref_id       — индекс в исходном списке

Сортировка:
    1. ts          — хронология
    2. chain_order — внутри одного ts: сначала разведка, потом C2
    3. subject     — стабильность
    4. type        — финальный tie-break

Детерминированно: одинаковый вход → побитово одинаковый список.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from paradox.models import Finding


# Маппинг тип finding → kill-chain фаза.
# Тип вне мапы → phase="other", chain_order=999 (в конце).
_PHASE_BY_TYPE: dict[str, str] = {
    # Reconnaissance
    "port_scan": "recon",
    "web_scanner": "recon",

    # Initial Access
    "vulnerable_path": "initial_access",
    "brute_force": "initial_access",
    "ftp_brute_force": "initial_access",
    "ssh_brute_force": "initial_access",
    "typosquatting": "initial_access",

    # Execution
    "command_injection": "execution",
    "script_download": "execution",
    "carved_file": "execution",

    # Command and Control
    "beaconing": "c2",
    "dns_tunneling": "c2",
    "dga_domain": "c2",
    "cloud_tunnel": "c2",
    "cloud_tunnel_domain": "c2",
    "ja3_malware": "c2",
    "ip_check_service": "c2",
    "sni_without_dns": "c2",
    "sni_mismatch": "c2",
    "suspicious_port": "c2",
    "nxdomain_burst": "c2",

    # Lateral Movement
    "lateral_movement": "lateral_movement",

    # Exfiltration
    "large_transfer": "exfiltration",

    # Impact
    "smtp_spam": "impact",
}

# Численный порядок фаз. Чем меньше — тем раньше в kill-chain.
_PHASE_ORDER: dict[str, int] = {
    "recon":             10,
    "initial_access":    20,
    "execution":         30,
    "persistence":       40,
    "lateral_movement":  50,
    "c2":                60,
    "exfiltration":      70,
    "impact":            80,
    "other":             999,
}

# Маппинг rule contradictions → фаза.
_PHASE_BY_RULE: dict[str, str] = {
    "victim_makes_attack":    "lateral_movement",
    "mac_ip_conflict":        "initial_access",  # ARP-spoof = MITM
    "sni_host_mismatch":      "c2",
    "ja3_ua_mismatch":        "c2",
    "reverse_asymmetry":      "exfiltration",
    "port_service_mismatch":  "c2",
    "direct_ip_no_dns":       "c2",
    "sni_without_dns":        "c2",
    "dns_answer_mismatch":    "c2",
    "sni_cert_mismatch":      "c2",
    "self_signed_cert":       "c2",
    "expired_cert":           "c2",
}


@dataclass
class TimelineEvent:
    ts: float
    kind: str              # "finding" | "contradiction"
    type: str
    subject: str
    severity: str
    confidence: float
    phase: str
    chain_order: int
    description: str
    mitre_id: str | None = None
    ref_id: int | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "ts": self.ts,
            "kind": self.kind,
            "type": self.type,
            "subject": self.subject,
            "severity": self.severity,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "phase": self.phase,
            "chain_order": self.chain_order,
            "description": self.description,
            "mitre_id": self.mitre_id,
            "ref_id": self.ref_id,
        }


def _phase_for_finding(ftype: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
    phase = _PHASE_BY_TYPE.get(ftype, "other")
    return phase, _PHASE_ORDER[phase]


def _phase_for_rule(rule: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
    phase = _PHASE_BY_RULE.get(rule, "other")
    return phase, _PHASE_ORDER[phase]


def _describe_finding(f: Finding) -> str:
    src = f.src_ip or "?"
    dst = f.dst_ip or "?"
    return f"{src} → {dst}: {f.type} ({f.value})"


def _describe_contradiction(c) -> str:
    rule = getattr(c, "rule", "?")
    subj = getattr(c, "subject", "?")
    hint = (getattr(c, "resolution_hint", None) or "").strip()
    if hint:
        return f"{subj}: {rule} — {hint[:120]}"
    return f"{subj}: {rule}"


def _describe_carved_file(cf) -> str:
    """Краткое описание payload'а для timeline."""
    uri = getattr(cf, "uri", None) or "?"
    size = getattr(cf, "size", 0)
    ext = getattr(cf, "ext", "") or ""
    sha = (getattr(cf, "sha256", "") or "")[:12]
    return f"{uri} ({ext}, {size} B, sha256 {sha}…)"


def _event_from_carved_file(cf, idx: int):
    """None если нет first_ts или нулевой."""
    ts = getattr(cf, "first_ts", None)
    if ts is None or ts <= 0:
        return None
    return TimelineEvent(
        ts=ts,
        kind="carved_file",
        type="carved_file",
        subject=getattr(cf, "dst_ip", "") or "",
        severity="medium",
        confidence=0.9,
        phase="execution",
        chain_order=_PHASE_ORDER["execution"],
        description=_describe_carved_file(cf),
        mitre_id="T1105",
        ref_id=idx,
    )


def _event_from_finding(f: Finding, idx: int) -> TimelineEvent | None:
    """None если нет first_seen — сортировать нечем."""
    if f.first_seen is None:
        return None
    phase, order = _phase_for_finding(f.type)
    return TimelineEvent(
        ts=f.first_seen,
        kind="finding",
        type=f.type,
        subject=f.src_ip or "",
        severity=f.severity,
        confidence=f.confidence,
        phase=phase,
        chain_order=order,
        description=_describe_finding(f),
        mitre_id=f.mitre_id,
        ref_id=idx,
    )


def _event_from_contradiction(c, idx: int) -> TimelineEvent | None:
    """
    ts = max(facts.ts) — момент, когда последний подтверждающий
    факт уже произошёл. Раньше не было бы видно противоречия.
    """
    facts = getattr(c, "facts", None) or []
    if not facts:
        return None
    ts_values = [getattr(f, "ts", 0.0) or 0.0 for f in facts]
    ts = max(ts_values) if ts_values else 0.0
    if ts <= 0.0:
        return None
    rule = getattr(c, "rule", "")
    phase, order = _phase_for_rule(rule)
    return TimelineEvent(
        ts=ts,
        kind="contradiction",
        type=rule,
        subject=getattr(c, "subject", ""),
        severity=getattr(c, "severity", "medium"),
        confidence=float(getattr(c, "confidence", 0.5)),
        phase=phase,
        chain_order=order,
        description=_describe_contradiction(c),
        mitre_id=None,
        ref_id=idx,
    )


def build_timeline(
    findings: list[Finding],
    contradictions: list | None = None,
    carved_files: list | None = None,
) -> list[TimelineEvent]:
    """
    Собрать timeline из findings, contradictions и carved_files.

    - Findings без first_seen пропускаются.
    - Contradictions без facts или с ts <= 0 пропускаются.
    - Carved files без first_ts пропускаются.
    - Сортировка: ts → chain_order → subject → type → kind.

    Детерминированно: одинаковый вход → одинаковый список.
    """
    events: list[TimelineEvent] = []
    for i, f in enumerate(findings):
        ev = _event_from_finding(f, i)
        if ev is not None:
            events.append(ev)
    if contradictions:
        for i, c in enumerate(contradictions):
            ev = _event_from_contradiction(c, i)
            if ev is not None:
                events.append(ev)
    if carved_files:
        for i, cf in enumerate(carved_files):
            ev = _event_from_carved_file(cf, i)
            if ev is not None:
                events.append(ev)

    events.sort(key=lambda e: (
        e.ts,
        e.chain_order,
        e.subject,
        e.type,
        e.kind,
    ))
    return events


def summarize_by_phase(events: list[TimelineEvent]) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Сгруппировать события по фазам kill-chain.

    Возвращает список dict'ов, отсортированный по chain_order:
        [{"phase": "recon", "chain_order": 10, "count": N,
          "events": [<dict>, ...]}, ...]
    """
    by_phase: dict[str, list[TimelineEvent]] = {}
    for ev in events:
        by_phase.setdefault(ev.phase, []).append(ev)
    out: list[dict] = []
    for phase, evs in by_phase.items():
        out.append({
            "phase": phase,
            "chain_order": _PHASE_ORDER.get(phase, 999),
            "count": len(evs),
            "events": [e.to_dict() for e in evs],
        })
    out.sort(key=lambda d: d["chain_order"])
    return out


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/tls_cert.py
# ==========================================
"""
Парсер TLS Certificate handshake message из TCP-потока сервера.

Структура:

  Record layer:
    [content_type(1)=0x16] [version(2)] [length(2)] [handshake_msg...]

  Handshake layer:
    [hs_type(1)] [length(3)] [body]

  Certificate body (TLS 1.2):
    [chain_length(3)]
    repeat: [cert_length(3)] [cert_der...]

  Certificate body (TLS 1.3):
    [request_context_length(1)] [request_context]
    [chain_length(3)]
    repeat: [cert_length(3)] [cert_der...]
    [extensions_length(2)] [extensions...]

Проблема: различить TLS 1.2 и 1.3 по префиксу нельзя. Для TLS 1.2
body[0] тоже 0 (старший байт 3-байтовой длины цепочки). Решение —
пробовать оба layout'а и брать первый, где DER-парсинг успешен.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from paradox.x509_cert import Certificate, parse_certificate
from paradox.x509_der import DerError


TLS_RECORD_HANDSHAKE = 0x16
HS_TYPE_CERTIFICATE = 11


def parse_certificate_from_stream(stream: bytes) -> Certificate | None:
    """Извлечь leaf-сертификат из TCP-потока сервер→клиент.

    Возвращает None, если TLS-хендшейк не найден или битый.
    Не бросает исключений на входных данных.
    """
    pos = 0
    while pos + 5 <= len(stream):
        content_type = stream[pos]
        if content_type != TLS_RECORD_HANDSHAKE:
            return None

        length = int.from_bytes(stream[pos + 3:pos + 5], "big")
        rec_start = pos + 5
        rec_end = rec_start + length
        if rec_end > len(stream):
            return None

        record = stream[rec_start:rec_end]
        for cert_der in _find_certificate_candidates(record):
            try:
                return parse_certificate(cert_der)
            except DerError:
                continue

        pos = rec_end

    return None


def _find_certificate_candidates(record: bytes) -> list[bytes]:
    """Все возможные DER-байты leaf-сертификата из одного record.

    Возвращает список кандидатов (сначала TLS 1.3, потом TLS 1.2).
    Вызывающий код пробует каждый через parse_certificate и берёт
    первый валидный.
    """
    pos = 0
    while pos + 4 <= len(record):
        hs_type = record[pos]
        hs_len = int.from_bytes(record[pos + 1:pos + 4], "big")
        body_start = pos + 4
        body_end = body_start + hs_len
        if body_end > len(record):
            return []

        if hs_type == HS_TYPE_CERTIFICATE:
            body = record[body_start:body_end]
            candidates: list[bytes] = []
            c13 = _try_extract_tls13(body)
            if c13 is not None:
                candidates.append(c13)
            c12 = _try_extract_tls12(body)
            if c12 is not None:
                candidates.append(c12)
            return candidates

        pos = body_end

    return []


def _try_extract_tls12(body: bytes) -> bytes | None:
    """TLS 1.2 layout: [chain_len(3)] [cert_len(3)] [der]..."""
    if len(body) < 6:
        return None
    chain_len = int.from_bytes(body[0:3], "big")
    if 3 + chain_len > len(body):
        return None
    cert_len = int.from_bytes(body[3:6], "big")
    if cert_len == 0 or 6 + cert_len > len(body):
        return None
    return body[6:6 + cert_len]


def _try_extract_tls13(body: bytes) -> bytes | None:
    """TLS 1.3 layout: [ctx_len(1)=0] [chain_len(3)] [cert_len(3)] [der]..."""
    if len(body) < 7:
        return None
    if body[0] != 0:
        return None
    chain_len = int.from_bytes(body[1:4], "big")
    if 4 + chain_len > len(body):
        return None
    cert_len = int.from_bytes(body[4:7], "big")
    if cert_len == 0 or 7 + cert_len > len(body):
        return None
    return body[7:7 + cert_len]


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/tls_parse.py
# ==========================================
"""
Парсер TLS ClientHello.

Извлекает SNI (Server Name Indication) — домен, к которому клиент
устанавливает TLS-соединение. Работает на сырых байтах TLS-записи.

Формат (RFC 8446, раздел 4):

  TLS record:
    content_type    1 байт   (0x16 = handshake)
    version         2 байта  (0x0301, 0x0303, ...)
    length          2 байта

  Handshake:
    msg_type        1 байт   (0x01 = ClientHello)
    length          3 байта
    body            ...

  ClientHello body:
    client_version  2 байта
    random          32 байта
    session_id_len  1 байт
    session_id      N байт
    cipher_suites_len 2 байта
    cipher_suites   N байт
    compression_len 1 байт
    compression     N байт
    extensions_len  2 байта
    extensions      N байт

  server_name extension (type 0x0000):
    server_name_list_len  2 байта
    name_type             1 байт   (0x00 = host_name)
    name_len              2 байта
    name                  N байт   ← это SNI

Ограничения:
- Не собирает фрагментированные TLS-записи (следующий шаг —
  StreamReassembler).
- Не поддерживает TLS 1.3 ECH (Encrypted Client Hello).
- Работает только с первой TLS-записью в payload.
"""
from __future__ import annotations


# content types
TLS_CONTENT_HANDSHAKE = 0x16

# handshake types
TLS_HANDSHAKE_CLIENT_HELLO = 0x01

# extension types
TLS_EXTENSION_SERVER_NAME = 0x0000

# server name types
TLS_SERVER_NAME_HOST = 0x00


def extract_sni(payload: bytes) -> str | None:
    """Извлечь SNI из payload TCP-пакета.

    Возвращает домен в lower case или None, если payload не содержит
    ClientHello с валидной server_name extension.
    """
    if not payload or len(payload) < 5:
        return None

    # --- TLS record ---
    if payload[0] != TLS_CONTENT_HANDSHAKE:
        return None
    # payload[1:3] — версия записи, не проверяем строго (может быть 0x0301)
    record_len = int.from_bytes(payload[3:5], "big")
    record_end = 5 + record_len
    if record_end > len(payload):
        # Запись обрезана — не наш случай (нужен reassembly)
        return None

    # --- handshake ---
    if len(payload) < 9:
        return None
    if payload[5] != TLS_HANDSHAKE_CLIENT_HELLO:
        return None
    handshake_len = int.from_bytes(payload[6:9], "big")
    body_start = 9
    body_end = body_start + handshake_len
    if body_end > len(payload):
        return None

    # --- ClientHello body ---
    pos = body_start
    if pos + 2 + 32 > body_end:
        return None
    pos += 2          # client_version
    pos += 32         # random

    # session_id
    if pos >= body_end:
        return None
    session_id_len = payload[pos]
    pos += 1 + session_id_len
    if pos > body_end:
        return None

    # cipher_suites
    if pos + 2 > body_end:
        return None
    cipher_suites_len = int.from_bytes(payload[pos:pos + 2], "big")
    pos += 2 + cipher_suites_len
    if pos > body_end:
        return None

    # compression_methods
    if pos >= body_end:
        return None
    compression_len = payload[pos]
    pos += 1 + compression_len
    if pos > body_end:
        return None

    # extensions
    if pos + 2 > body_end:
        return None
    extensions_len = int.from_bytes(payload[pos:pos + 2], "big")
    pos += 2
    extensions_end = pos + extensions_len
    if extensions_end > body_end:
        return None

    return _find_sni_extension(payload, pos, extensions_end)


def _find_sni_extension(
    data: bytes, start: int, end: int,
) -> str | None:
    """Пройти по extensions, найти server_name (0x0000)."""
    pos = start
    while pos + 4 <= end:
        ext_type = int.from_bytes(data[pos:pos + 2], "big")
        ext_len = int.from_bytes(data[pos + 2:pos + 4], "big")
        ext_data_start = pos + 4
        ext_data_end = ext_data_start + ext_len
        if ext_data_end > end:
            return None

        if ext_type == TLS_EXTENSION_SERVER_NAME:
            return _parse_server_name(data, ext_data_start, ext_data_end)

        pos = ext_data_end

    return None


def _parse_server_name(
    data: bytes, start: int, end: int,
) -> str | None:
    """Разобрать server_name extension."""
    if start + 2 > end:
        return None
    list_len = int.from_bytes(data[start:start + 2], "big")
    pos = start + 2
    list_end = pos + list_len
    if list_end > end:
        return None

    while pos + 3 <= list_end:
        name_type = data[pos]
        name_len = int.from_bytes(data[pos + 1:pos + 3], "big")
        name_start = pos + 3
        name_end = name_start + name_len
        if name_end > list_end:
            return None

        if name_type == TLS_SERVER_NAME_HOST:
            try:
                name = data[name_start:name_end].decode("ascii")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                return None
            return name.lower()

        pos = name_end

    return None


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/x509_cert.py
# ==========================================
"""
Извлечение полей из X.509 Certificate поверх x509_der.

Что берём:
  - subject_cn: последний CN в Subject
  - issuer_cn:  последний CN в Issuer
  - is_self_signed: issuer_raw == subject_raw (байтовое равенство)
  - not_before / not_after: из Validity (ISO-строки)
  - san_dns / san_ips: из extension 2.5.29.17

Чего НЕ делаем:
  - не валидируем подпись (нужен публичный ключ issuer)
  - не разбираем certificate chain (только отдельный сертификат)
  - не парсим KeyUsage, ExtendedKeyUsage, BasicConstraints и т.п.
    (для триажа не нужно; добавим по мере правил)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.x509_der import (
    DerError,
    DerNode,
    TAG_CTX_0,
    TAG_CTX_3,
    TAG_OCTET_STRING,
    TAG_OID,
    TAG_SEQUENCE,
    as_oid,
    as_str,
    as_time,
    parse_der,
)


# OID, которые нам нужны
CN_OID = "2.5.4.3"          # commonName (2.5.4.3)
SAN_OID = "2.5.29.17"       # subjectAltName


@dataclass
class Certificate:
    subject_cn: str | None = None
    issuer_cn: str | None = None
    is_self_signed: bool = False
    not_before: str | None = None
    not_after: str | None = None
    san_dns: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    san_ips: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


def parse_certificate(der: bytes) -> Certificate:
    """Распарсить DER сертификата. Бросает DerError на структурных ошибках."""
    root = parse_der(der)
    if root.tag != TAG_SEQUENCE or not root.children:
        raise DerError(
            f"Certificate: expected SEQUENCE, got tag 0x{root.tag:02X}"
        )

    tbs = root.children[0]
    if tbs.tag != TAG_SEQUENCE or not tbs.children:
        raise DerError("TBSCertificate: expected SEQUENCE")

    # version [0] EXPLICIT — если есть, сдвигаем индексы на 1
    idx = 1 if tbs.children[0].tag == TAG_CTX_0 else 0

    # [idx] serialNumber, [idx+1] signature, [idx+2] issuer,
    # [idx+3] validity, [idx+4] subject, [idx+5] subjectPublicKeyInfo
    if len(tbs.children) < idx + 5:
        raise DerError(
            f"TBSCertificate: too few fields ({len(tbs.children)})"
        )

    issuer_node = tbs.children[idx + 2]
    validity_node = tbs.children[idx + 3]
    subject_node = tbs.children[idx + 4]

    issuer_cn = _extract_last_cn(issuer_node)
    subject_cn = _extract_last_cn(subject_node)

    # Self-signed: побайтовое сравнение DER-содержимого Name.
    # Обычно совпадает точно, если issuer и subject идентичны.
    is_self_signed = issuer_node.value == subject_node.value

    not_before, not_after = _parse_validity(validity_node)

    san_dns: list[str] = []
    san_ips: list[str] = []
    for child in tbs.children[idx + 5:]:
        if child.tag == TAG_CTX_3:
            san_dns, san_ips = _parse_extensions_for_san(child)
            break

    return Certificate(
        subject_cn=subject_cn,
        issuer_cn=issuer_cn,
        is_self_signed=is_self_signed,
        not_before=not_before,
        not_after=not_after,
        san_dns=san_dns,
        san_ips=san_ips,
    )


def _extract_last_cn(name_node: DerNode) -> str | None:
    """Последний CN из RDNSequence. None если CN нет."""
    cn: str | None = None
    for rdn in name_node.children:
        for attr in rdn.children:
            if len(attr.children) < 2:
                continue
            try:
                if attr.children[0].tag != TAG_OID:
                    continue
                if as_oid(attr.children[0]) != CN_OID:
                    continue
                cn = as_str(attr.children[1])
            except DerError:
                continue
    return cn


def _parse_validity(node: DerNode) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
    if len(node.children) < 2:
        return None, None
    nb = as_time(node.children[0])
    na = as_time(node.children[1])
    return (
        nb[0] if nb else None,
        na[0] if na else None,
    )


def _parse_extensions_for_san(
    ext_node: DerNode,
) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    """Extensions [3] EXPLICIT → SEQUENCE OF Extension. Найти SAN."""
    if not ext_node.children:
        return [], []
    # ext_node.children[0] — SEQUENCE OF Extension
    seq = ext_node.children[0]
    for ext in seq.children:
        if len(ext.children) < 2:
            continue
        try:
            if ext.children[0].tag != TAG_OID:
                continue
            if as_oid(ext.children[0]) != SAN_OID:
                continue
        except DerError:
            continue
        for c in ext.children[1:]:
            if c.tag == TAG_OCTET_STRING:
                return _parse_san(c.value)
    return [], []


def _parse_san(der_bytes: bytes) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    """SubjectAltName = SEQUENCE OF GeneralName.

    dNSName     — context [2] IA5String (тег 0x82)
    iPAddress   — context [7] OCTET STRING (тег 0x87)
    Остальные GeneralName (email, URI, directory) игнорируем — не нужны.
    """
    dns_names: list[str] = []
    ips: list[str] = []
    try:
        root = parse_der(der_bytes)
    except DerError:
        return dns_names, ips

    if root.tag != TAG_SEQUENCE:
        return dns_names, ips

    for gn in root.children:
        if gn.tag_class != 2:
            continue
        low_tag = gn.tag & 0x1F

        if low_tag == 2:  # dNSName
            name = gn.value.decode("ascii", errors="ignore").strip()
            if name:
                dns_names.append(name)

        elif low_tag == 7:  # iPAddress
            if len(gn.value) == 4:
                ips.append(".".join(str(b) for b in gn.value))
            elif len(gn.value) == 16:
                parts = [
                    f"{gn.value[i]:02x}{gn.value[i + 1]:02x}"
                    for i in range(0, 16, 2)
                ]
                ips.append(":".join(parts))

    return dns_names, ips


# ==========================================
# FILE: paradox/x509_der.py
# ==========================================
"""
Минимальный DER-парсер для X.509. Только чтение, без валидации.

DER — это ASN.1 с ограничениями:
  - definite-length (никогда indefinite)
  - только primitive SEQUENCE/SET (никаких constructed строк)
  - уникальные тэги в SET (для нас неважно)

Формат: [tag(1)] [length(1..5)] [value(length)]
  - tag: 0x30 = SEQUENCE, 0x31 = SET, 0x06 = OID, 0x02 = INTEGER, ...
  - length:
    - < 0x80 — короткий, значение длины прямо в байте
    - >= 0x80 — long form: младшие 7 бит = число байт длины
  - value — вложенная структура или примитив

Зачем свой:
  - проект держит scapy только для DNS, тут своё
  - нам нужны 4 поля: subject CN, issuer, validity, SAN
  - полный X.509 — 2000+ LOC, избыточно

Ограничения:
  - не поддерживаем indefinite-length (0x80) — X.509 его не использует
  - не поддерживаем high-tag-number (tag & 0x1F == 0x1F) — в X.509
    не встречается, кроме редких extension'ов
  - ошибки → DerError, не падаем молча
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field


# === Константы тэгов (universal class, low-tag) ===

TAG_BOOLEAN = 0x01
TAG_INTEGER = 0x02
TAG_BIT_STRING = 0x03
TAG_OCTET_STRING = 0x04
TAG_NULL = 0x05
TAG_OID = 0x06
TAG_UTF8_STRING = 0x0C
TAG_SEQUENCE = 0x30
TAG_SET = 0x31
TAG_PRINTABLE_STRING = 0x13
TAG_IA5_STRING = 0x16
TAG_UTC_TIME = 0x17
TAG_GENERALIZED_TIME = 0x18

# Контекстные тэги (используются в X.509 extensions и в SAN)
# Class 0b10 (context-specific), constructed = bit 0x20
TAG_CTX_0 = 0xA0
TAG_CTX_1 = 0xA1
TAG_CTX_2 = 0xA2
TAG_CTX_3 = 0xA3


class DerError(ValueError):
    """Некорректный DER — длина не сходится, тэг не поддерживается."""


@dataclass
class DerNode:
    """Узел DER-дерева.

    tag             — сырой байт тэга (0x30, 0x06, 0xA2, ...)
    tag_class       — 0=universal, 1=application, 2=context, 3=private
    constructed     — True для SEQUENCE/SET/context-constructed
    value           — сырые байты значения (для primitive) или всего
                      вложенного содержимого (для constructed)
    children        — распарсенные вложенные узлы (только constructed)
    """
    tag: int
    tag_class: int
    constructed: bool
    value: bytes
    children: list["DerNode"] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def is_primitive(self) -> bool:
        return not self.constructed

    @property
    def is_universal(self) -> bool:
        return self.tag_class == 0


def _parse_length(data: bytes, pos: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Вернуть (length, new_pos). Бросает DerError, если данных мало."""
    if pos >= len(data):
        raise DerError("length: unexpected end of data")
    first = data[pos]
    if first < 0x80:
        return first, pos + 1
    num_bytes = first & 0x7F
    if num_bytes == 0:
        raise DerError("indefinite length not supported in DER")
    if num_bytes > 4:
        raise DerError(f"length too large: {num_bytes} bytes")
    if pos + 1 + num_bytes > len(data):
        raise DerError("length: truncated long-form")
    length = int.from_bytes(data[pos + 1:pos + 1 + num_bytes], "big")
    return length, pos + 1 + num_bytes


def parse_der(data: bytes) -> DerNode:
    """Распарсить один DER-объект из начала data.

    Возвращает корневой узел. Остаток data игнорируется —
    X.509 сертификат занимает всё, что передали, но при вложенных
    вызовах (parse внутри parse) мы работаем с куском.
    """
    node, _end = _parse_one(data, 0)
    return node


def _parse_one(data: bytes, pos: int) -> tuple[DerNode, int]:
    """Распарсить один TLV начиная с pos. Вернуть (node, new_pos)."""
    if pos >= len(data):
        raise DerError("tag: unexpected end of data")

    tag = data[pos]
    tag_class = (tag >> 6) & 0x03
    constructed = bool(tag & 0x20)
    low_tag = tag & 0x1F

    if low_tag == 0x1F:
        raise DerError("high-tag-number form not supported")

    length, val_start = _parse_length(data, pos + 1)
    val_end = val_start + length
    if val_end > len(data):
        raise DerError(
            f"value truncated: need {length}, have {len(data) - val_start}"
        )

    value = data[val_start:val_end]
    children: list[DerNode] = []

    if constructed:
        inner_pos = 0
        while inner_pos < len(value):
            child, inner_pos = _parse_one(value, inner_pos)
            children.append(child)

    return DerNode(
        tag=tag,
        tag_class=tag_class,
        constructed=constructed,
        value=value,
        children=children,
    ), val_end


# === Хелперы для примитивов ===

def as_int(node: DerNode) -> int:
    """INTEGER → int. В DER всегда big-endian, знаковый."""
    if node.tag != TAG_INTEGER:
        raise DerError(f"expected INTEGER (0x02), got 0x{node.tag:02X}")
    return int.from_bytes(node.value, "big", signed=True)


def as_oid(node: DerNode) -> str:
    """OID → строковое представление '2.5.29.17'.

    DER-кодирование OID: первая пара (a.b) упакована в один байт
    (a * 40 + b), затем base-128 с continuation bit.
    """
    if node.tag != TAG_OID:
        raise DerError(f"expected OID (0x06), got 0x{node.tag:02X}")
    if not node.value:
        raise DerError("empty OID")

    # Первый байт: a * 40 + b, где a ∈ {0,1,2}
    first = node.value[0]
    if first < 40:
        arcs = [0, first]
    elif first < 80:
        arcs = [1, first - 40]
    else:
        arcs = [2, first - 80]

    # Остальные — base-128 с продолжением
    val = 0
    for byte in node.value[1:]:
        val = (val << 7) | (byte & 0x7F)
        if not (byte & 0x80):
            arcs.append(val)
            val = 0
    if val:
        raise DerError("truncated OID continuation")

    return ".".join(str(a) for a in arcs)


def as_str(node: DerNode) -> str:
    """Строковые типы → str. UTF8String / PrintableString / IA5String."""
    if node.tag not in (
        TAG_UTF8_STRING, TAG_PRINTABLE_STRING, TAG_IA5_STRING,
    ):
        raise DerError(
            f"expected string, got tag 0x{node.tag:02X}"
        )
    return node.value.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")


def as_time(node: DerNode) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
    """UTCTime / GeneralizedTime → ISO-строка 'YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS'.

    UTCTime: YYMMDDHHMMSSZ (или YYMMDDHHMMZ)
    GeneralizedTime: YYYYMMDDHHMMSSZ

    Возвращает None, если формат неизвестен — не падаем на битых cert.
    """
    if node.tag == TAG_UTC_TIME:
        s = node.value.decode("ascii", errors="ignore").strip()
        if len(s) < 12:
            return None
        # YY → YYYY (по RFC 5280: 50-99 → 1950-1999, 00-49 → 2000-2049)
        yy = int(s[0:2])
        year = 1900 + yy if yy >= 50 else 2000 + yy
        try:
            month, day = int(s[2:4]), int(s[4:6])
            hour, minute = int(s[6:8]), int(s[8:10])
            # YYMMDDHHMMSSZ = 13 символов; YYMMDDHHMMZ = 11 (без сек).
            # Раньше было >= 14 — резало секунды в 0.
            sec = int(s[10:12]) if len(s) >= 12 else 0
        except ValueError:
            return None
        return (
            f"{year:04d}-{month:02d}-{day:02d}T{hour:02d}:{minute:02d}:{sec:02d}",
            "utc",
        )

    if node.tag == TAG_GENERALIZED_TIME:
        s = node.value.decode("ascii", errors="ignore").strip()
        if len(s) < 14:
            return None
        try:
            year = int(s[0:4])
            month, day = int(s[4:6]), int(s[6:8])
            hour, minute, sec = int(s[8:10]), int(s[10:12]), int(s[12:14])
        except ValueError:
            return None
        return (
            f"{year:04d}-{month:02d}-{day:02d}T{hour:02d}:{minute:02d}:{sec:02d}",
            "generalized",
        )

    return None
